### [1] [Beyond Textual Chain-of-Thought: A Survey on Action-Grounded Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01659)
*Zhengxu Tang,Xiaozhou Zhang,Guofeng Cui,Ziyu Gong,Zi Wang,Yunfei Shi,Ruifeng Deng,Chengzhi Qi,Ke Chen,Sachin Patil,Tianjun Xiao,Langechuan Liu,Pichao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本综述系统研究了自动驾驶中从文本思维链（CoT）到动作落地推理的转变，通过调研171篇论文，提出以中间表示形式为核心的分类体系，将130种方法归纳为语言推理、视觉空间推理、潜在动态推理和外部化推理四大类，并细分为13个子类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理通过生成中间步骤来提升生成式模型的能力，但在自动驾驶领域，最终输出是连续动作，推理过程必须与物理世界的时空结构保持一致。因此需要从"文本思维链"转向"动作落地的推理"（action-grounded reasoning），但目前该领域缺乏系统性的梳理与分类框架。

Method: 对171篇论文（包含130篇方法论文和41篇基准/数据集/综述/分析论文）进行全面调研，提出以表示为中心的（representation-centered）分类体系，将中间状态的表示形式作为组织轴，把130种方法分为语言推理、视觉空间推理、潜在动态推理和外部化推理四大类别，并进一步细分为13个子类型。

Result: 实验表明，驾驶智能体中推理的前沿在于能够落地于真实世界的中间表示、与实时动作耦合、并在安全关键系统下可被验证的推理方法。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶推理研究的开放前沿在于设计能真实世界落地、支持实时动作生成、且可在安全关键系统下验证的中间表示。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning powers generative models by eliciting intermediate steps before producing an answer. In autonomous driving, the answer is a continuous action. Thus its reasoning must share the same spatiotemporal structure as the physical world. This survey studies the resulting shift from textual CoT to action-grounded reasoning. Surveying 171 papers, including 130 method papers and 41 benchmarks, datasets, surveys, and analysis papers, we propose a representation-centered taxonomy that treats the form of the intermediate state as the organizing axis. We systematize the 130 methods into four categories: language-based, visual-spatial, latent-dynamic, and externalized reasoning, further divided into 13 subtypes tied to distinct regions of interests. Our synthesis shows that the open frontier of reasoning in driving agents lies in intermediate representations that can be grounded in the real world, coupled to real-time action, and verified under safety-critical systems. Project page: https://github.com/tangzhengxu/awesome-av-cot.

</details>


### [2] [FORGE: Forward-Only Test-Time Adaptation for Integer-Only Vision Models on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01683)
*Muhammad Rehan,Haider Ali,Muhammad Ali Munir,Moaz Amjad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于真实微控制器（MCU）上整数-only、BN折叠卷积网络的纯前向测试时自适应（TTA）方法。关键洞察是BN融合进卷积破坏归一化统计信息，该方法通过仅使用前向估计重新归一化每个折叠卷积的逐通道输出到训练统计来恢复自适应能力。方法在ESP32-S3上验证位精确int8卷积执行，前向自适应仅需8.3mJ（推理能耗的6.8%）和21.9ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法要么只适用于服务器或边缘GPU级别模型（非真正MCU整数执行），要么依赖批归一化（BN）层——而整数部署会将BN折叠融合进卷积而移除，导致依赖BN统计的归一化自适应方法失效。需要一种能在部署后的BN折叠整数-only模型上运行的纯前向TTA方法。

Method: 核心方法：对每个折叠卷积的逐通道输出，用纯前向估计量重新归一化到其训练时的干净统计量（均值/方差近似）。无需梯度回传，仅靠前向传播的统计估计实现自适应。针对单样本流式场景使用批量大小缩放的动量，并仅需选择3/21层进行自适应（无需看到测试腐败即可选定）。fp32重校准轻量计算包绕在int8卷积周围。

Result: (i) 恢复梯度方法TENT大部分精度增益（+20.9 vs +24.9点），匹配纯前向BN自适应方法，且是唯一能在折叠整数-only模型上运行的方法；(ii) 仅需适配3/21层即可恢复93%收益；(iii) 在单样本流式场景下通过批量缩放动量保持鲁棒性；(iv) 在3个数据集（最多200类）和2种架构上泛化。在ESP32-S3上代价仅8.3mJ和21.9ms。

Conclusion: 纯前向自适应在真实微控制器上廉价且可行，填补了MCU端部署模型在分布偏移下无法自适应的空白。整数-only部署的归一化统计可以通过前向重校准恢复，使得MCU上的模型在测试时也具备环境偏移鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision models deployed on microcontrollers (MCUs) are quantized to integer-only arithmetic and run in inference-only runtimes that do not carry the machinery backpropagation needs: the standard tool for adapting a model to the distribution shift (sensor noise, blur, lighting) it meets in the field. Existing forward-only test-time adaptation (TTA) methods either run only on server- or edge-GPU-class models (not true microcontroller integer execution), or require the batch-normalization (BN) layers that integer deployment fuses away. We present a forward-only TTA method that operates on deployed, BN-folded, integer-only convolutional networks. The key observation is that fusing BN into the preceding convolution, a mandatory step for integer inference, destroys the statistics that normalization-based adaptation relies on. We restore adaptation by re-normalizing each folded convolution's per-channel output to its clean training statistics, using only forward-pass estimates. The method (i) recovers most of gradient-based TENT's accuracy gain (+20.9 vs. +24.9 points) and matches forward-only BN adaptation, while being the only method that runs on a folded integer-only model; (ii) needs to adapt only 3 of 21 layers (selected without seeing the test corruptions) to recover 93% of the benefit; (iii) survives single-sample streaming with a batch-size-scaled momentum; and (iv) generalizes across three datasets (up to 200 classes) and two architectures. We validate bit-exact int8 convolution execution and deploy on an ESP32-S3, where, measured with a Nordic PPK2 power profiler, the forward-only adaptation (a lightweight fp32 recalibration around the int8 convolutions) costs only 8.3 mJ (6.8% of inference energy) and 21.9 ms on the deployed SIMD-optimized model: forward-only adaptation is cheap on a real microcontroller.

</details>


### [3] [FairLens: Benchmarking Fairness in Vision-Language Models for High-Stakes Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01691)
*Vahid Reza Khazaie,Ahmed Y. Radwan,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAIRLENS是一个用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在高风险领域（招聘、法律、医疗）中公平性与有效性的基准和评估框架。它结合真实人脸图像（涵盖性别、种族和年龄组）与开放式/封闭式问题，从四个互补视角评估模型响应。对8个VLM的评估发现，主要失败模式是无根据推断（如从面部推断资格、疾病、威胁或职业角色）而非不平等对待。在绝对差距很小但基线不良率低时，即使小的群体差距也可能导致一个族群被贴上不良标签的频率是另一个的数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM越来越多地用于基于视觉输入做出高风险决策，现有的评估多关注于整体准确性或简单的群体公平指标，缺乏系统性衡量高影响领域中VLM响应的公平性与有效性（即响应是否基于证据而非无根据推断）的框架。特别是在法律和医疗等识别证据不足至关重要的领域，仅靠分歧度量（parity）可能遗漏模型对每个群体都不安全处理的问题。

Method: FAIRLENS将覆盖性别、种族和年龄的真实人脸图像与封闭式和开放式问题配对，构成每个模型超过10万图像-问题对，并从四个互补视角进行评估：(1) 不良结果率的群体均等性（demographic parity）；(2) 合理性（soundness）——响应遵循问题中陈述的证据并在图像无法支撑答案时弃权；(3) 对无支撑的角色和状态的群体关联；(4) 自由文本生成中的偏见。评估了8个VLM。

Result: 主要失败是无根据推断而非不平等对待——模型经常从面部推断资格、威胁、疾病或职业角色，最弱模型在其输入无法回答的99%问题上做出这种推断。这类失败在法律和医疗领域最为严重。均等性差距绝对值较小，但当基线不良率低时，相同差距意味着一个群体被赋予不良标签的频率是另一个的数倍。自由文本中的偏见与多项选择的准确性仅松散耦合，正确的结构化回答不代表安全生成。

Conclusion: 高风险场景中公平的VLM行为需要同时满足两个条件：跨群体的平等对待以及拒绝从外貌推断高风险属性。仅依赖均等性度量会遗漏系统性的无根据推断问题。FAIRLENS的问题套件可转移至任何具有人口统计标注的人脸语料库，为VLM在高风险视觉决策中的安全部署提供了综合性评估工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to make decisions from visual inputs. We introduce FAIRLENS, a benchmark and evaluation framework for measuring both the fairness and the validity of VLM responses in three high-stakes domains: hiring, legal, and healthcare. FAIRLENS pairs real face images spanning gender, race, and age groups with closed- and open-ended questions, giving more than 100K image-question pairs per model, and evaluates responses from four complementary views: demographic parity over adverse outcome rates, soundness, demographic association over unsupported roles and statuses, and bias in free-text generation. Soundness is the central validity criterion: a response is sound when it follows the evidence stated in the question and abstains when the image cannot support an answer. Evaluating eight VLMs, we find that the primary failure is unwarranted inference rather than unequal treatment. Models routinely infer qualifications, threat, illness, or professional role from a face instead of abstaining, and the weakest model does so on 99% of the questions its input cannot answer. These failures are most severe in legal and healthcare, where recognizing insufficient evidence matters most, and disparity metrics alone would miss them: parity gaps are small in absolute terms, yet when baseline adverse rates are low the same gap means one demographic group receives adverse labels several times as often as another, and a small gap can equally reflect a model that treats every group unsafely. Bias in free-text responses is only loosely coupled to multiple-choice accuracy, so correct structured answers do not imply safe generation. FAIRLENS shows that fair high-stakes VLM behavior requires similar treatment across groups and refusal to infer high-stakes attributes from appearance, and its question suite transfers to any face corpus with demographic annotations.

</details>


### [4] [From Visual Cues to Spoken Narration: Rethinking Audio Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01725)
*Akshita Gupta,Aditya Arora,Federico Tombari,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cue2Narrate，一个两阶段的音频描述（AD）生成流水线，联合预测电影中的"叙说什么"（what）和"何时叙述"（when），解决了先前工作主要简化视频字幕生成而忽略时序定位的问题。该方法通过双头音视频定位器预测视频线索窗口和叙述窗口，结合LoRA适应的VLM和描述排序损失生成AD，并引入长片段的LongLSMDC基准进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频描述（AD）需要为视障观众选择合适的视觉事件（what）以及在对话间隙中的插入位置（when）。以往工作将AD问题简化为对预先分割视频片段的视频字幕生成，导致what被预定义、when完全被忽视，无法处理更长的未剪裁影片片段，也无法真正优化用户体验。

Method: Cue2Narrate采用两阶段流水线：（1）双头音频-视觉定位器对每条AD语句预测两个时间上不同的窗口——视觉线索窗口和叙述窗口；（2）利用LoRA适应的视觉语言模型（VLM）基于预测的视觉证据生成简洁AD，并通过描述排序损失训练，使同帧的负面样本描述排名低于真实AD。此外引入了包含最长8分钟（平均约6.5分钟）电影片段的LongLSMDC基准。

Result: 在LongLSMDC基准上，Cue2Narrate在平均mAP上超越仅视频和仅音频定位基线5—12个百分点。在预测窗口和真实窗口两种评估设置下，Cue2Narrate相比对应微调的基础VLM提升了AD生成质量。该工作建立了首个长形式多片段AD生成基准。

Conclusion: Cue2Narrate证明了联合建模"何时叙述"与"叙述什么"对长未剪裁影片AD生成的重要性，并提供了首个多片段AD生成的基准LongLSMDC以及公开的数据与代码资源，为该研究方向奠定基础。

Abstract: Audio Description (AD) provides spoken narration of visual events during dialogue gaps, making movies accessible to visually impaired audiences. The problem requires determining both what (which visual event) and when (position for inserting the AD) to narrate, to achieve the best user experience. Prior work has largely reduced the problem to video captioning of pre-segmented video clips, i.e., what is largely predefined and when is ignored entirely. We propose Cue2Narrate, a two-stage pipeline that jointly predicts what and when to narrate in longer untrimmed movie clips. A dual-head audio-visual localizer predicts two temporally distinct windows per AD utterance: a visual cue window and a spoken narration window. A LoRA-adapted VLM then generates concise ADs from the predicted visual evidence, trained with a Description Ranking Loss that ranks captions (negative samples) of the same frames lower than the GT AD. To benchmark this new problem statement, we introduce the LongLSMDC benchmark with up to 8-min movie clips (~6.5min on average). On LongLSMDC, Cue2Narrate outperforms video-only and audio-only localization baselines by 5--12 points in avg. mAP. Under both predicted- and GT-window evaluation, Cue2Narrate improves AD generation over the corresponding fine-tuned base VLM. These results establish the first benchmark for multi-segment AD generation on long-form clips. Data & Code: https://github.com/multimodal-ai-lab/Cue2Narrate

</details>


### [5] [UAV Thermal Imagery for Inert Ordnance Screening: Multi Campaign Dataset Development,Object Detection, and Practical Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01738)
*Chad Melton,PhD.,Annabelle Kelton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究创建了多批次无人机热成像的惰性未爆弹药（UXO）数据集，包含5,855个热成像标签对，并在33m和15m两种飞行高度下收集数据。训练了YOLOV11l和RT-DETR-R50两种检测算法，提出了针对人道主义排雷和扫雷应用中自动化候选目标检测的实际建议，旨在为后续技术调查提供筛查与优先级排序支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未爆弹药（UXO）持续限制着受污染地区的平民活动、农业生产、基础设施恢复和环境治理。需要开发有效的自动化检测方法来辅助人道主义排雷行动，提高调查效率和安全性。

Method: 在美国田纳西州开展了四次实地飞行战役，分别在夏季和冬季条件下收集数据，将惰性地雷、弹药等布设在短草、高植被、砾石、覆盖物、岩石、堆肥和压实等多种地表上。使用33m和15m飞行高度采集热成像，最终构建5,855个热图像标签对（918张正样本和4,937张背景图像）。对背景图像进行降采样后，33m数据集含420张训练和106张验证图像，15m数据集含629张训练和157张验证图像。训练并评估了YOLOV11l和RT-DETR-R50两种目标检测算法。

Result: 研究建立了包含多种地表类型、季节条件和飞行高度的热成像UXO数据集，成功训练并评估了YOLOV11l和RT-DETR-R50算法用于自动化候选目标检测。研究总结了一系列实用建议，包括联合采集热成像与RGB图像、纳入多样化地表和纯背景图像、考虑太阳辐射变化后的时段、平衡测绘覆盖范围与目标像素表示、使用代表性本地数据校准模型，以及保留合格的人工复核环节。

Conclusion: 该模型预期的应用场景是用于排查和优先级排序，为后续的技术调查或爆炸物处理（EOD）评估提供支持，而非作为独立的排雷清除手段。这将有助于提升人道主义排雷行动的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Unexploded ordnance (UXO) continues to restrict civilian access, agricultural activity, infrastructure recovery, and environmental remediation in contaminated areas around the world. This study created a multi campaign UAV thermal image data set of inert ordnance, developed a labeled image set from collected imagery, tested object detection models, and identified practical considerations for humanitarian mine action and demining applications. Data were collected during four field campaigns in Tennessee under summer and winter conditions using inert mines, munitions, and other ordnance placed in short grass, tall vegetation, gravel, mulch, rock, compost, and compacted surfaces. Thermal imagery was collected under flight altitutes of 33 m and 15 m. The final source inventory contained 5,855 thermal image label pairs, including 918 positive images and 4,937 background images. After retaining all positive images and downsampling background images, the 33 m dataset contained 420 training and 106 validation images, while the 15 m dataset contained 629 training and 157 validation images. YOLOV11l and RT-DETR-R50 algorithms were trained and evaluated to develop an automated candidate detection model. Practical recommendations include collecting thermal and RGB imagery together, incorporating varied surfaces and background only imagery, considering periods following changes in solar exposure, balancing survey coverage against target pixel representation, calibrating models with representative local data, and retaining qualified human review. The intended use is screening and prioritization for follow on technical survey or EOD assessment, and not a standalone clearance.

</details>


### [6] [Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution in Facial Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02751)
*Sheng Liu,Qiang Sheng,Danding Wang,Yu Li,Chenming Zhou,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多工具图像编辑归因（MIEA）任务，识别面部图像经过多种编辑工具处理后的来源工具。构建了包含50万+编辑图像的多工具编辑数据集MultiEdit，覆盖六种编辑工具（深度换脸及多种面部增强）。设计了DPEC方法，结合空间域和频率域的可区分局部痕迹感知，并通过基于错误的课程学习策略训练。实验表明DPEC在最多五步编辑的面部图像上优于九种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑归因方法基于单一工具假设，只能识别一种编辑工具，无法处理多工具编辑场景——不同工具留下的伪影是复合且重叠的。随着生成式AI工具日益强大易用，多工具编辑越来越普遍，这构成了当前研究的主要空白。

Method: 1) 提出MIEA任务定义多工具编辑归因问题；2) 构建MultiEdit数据集，包含50万+面部图像和六种编辑工具（深度换脸+各类面部增强），模拟真实编辑场景；3) 设计DPEC方法，从空间域和频率域捕获可区分且具有局部性感知的编辑工具痕迹，并采用基于错误的课程学习策略进行训练。

Result: DPEC在最多五步编辑的面部图像上，性能优于九种对比方法，验证了其在多工具编辑归因任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 多工具编辑归因是可行的且有价值的研究方向，DPEC通过空间/频率双域特征提取和课程学习策略能有效识别多个编辑工具的痕迹，为该领域提供了新任务定义、数据集和基线方法。

Abstract: As generative AI tools become increasingly powerful and easy to use, people can easily edit portrait images with a prompt, necessitating the task of image editing attribution, which predicts the involved editing tools from the given image. Existing attribution methods hold the single-tool assumption and can only attribute a specific editing tool, but struggle to handle the more complex and increasingly common multi-tool editing scenarios, where artifacts left by different editing tools are composite and overlapped. To address this gap, we explore Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution (MIEA), which aims to identify multiple editing tools involved in a multi-tool edited facial image. To simulate the real-life editing operations on facial images, we then construct a new dataset, MultiEdit, which contains 500k+ edited facial images and covers six types of editing tools that support face swapping (Deepfake) and various facial enhancements. Inspired by the findings from data analysis, we design DPEC, a multi-tool attribution method that can capture distinguishable, locality-aware editing tool traces from both spatial and frequency domains with the support of an error-based curriculum learning strategy. Experiments show \Method\ outperforms nine methods for facial images edited in at most five steps.

</details>


### [7] [ZipTok3D: High-Fidelity 3D Tokenization with Compact Token Prefixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01740)
*Mingda Lin,Weijie Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Bowen Cui,Yefei He,Haoyu Zhao,Yuanyu He,Donny Y. Chen,Feng Chen,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ZipTok3D，一种面向极短token序列的3D tokenizer，通过嵌套dropout将几何信息按优先级组织进token前缀，并用参数共享Transformer迭代解码实现高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D tokenizer在极低token预算下重建质量急剧退化，难以同时满足紧凑性和高保真重建需求。

Method: (1) 编码阶段使用嵌套dropout随机截断潜在序列，要求每个保留的前缀都能重建完整物体，使重要几何信息优先存入前导token；(2) 解码阶段通过迭代应用参数共享的Transformer块，从每个前缀逐步恢复细粒度几何，无需单独的生成采样阶段。

Result: 在相同token维度下，ZipTok3D用1个token即可达到COD-VAE基线32个token的重建质量（ShapeNet），用4个token达到其TRELLIS上的效果，分别实现32倍和8倍的序列压缩。

Conclusion: 通过前缀化的渐进信息组织和迭代解码机制，ZipTok3D能够从极短token序列实现高保真3D重建，显著缩短token序列长度。

Abstract: Compact token sequences are essential for efficient 3D generation. However, existing 3D tokenizers typically organize latent representations either over spatial regions or as fixed-size sets of global tokens, both suffering sharp reconstruction degradation when compressed to extremely low token budgets. In this paper, we present ZipTok3D, a 3D tokenizer designed for high-fidelity reconstruction from extremely short token sequences. Its key idea is to organize object geometry into progressively informative global-token prefixes and unfold these compact representations through iterative decoding. Specifically, nested dropout randomly truncates the latent sequence after encoding during training and requires each retained prefix to reconstruct the complete object, thereby prioritizing essential geometric information in the leading tokens. The decoder then repeatedly applies a parameter-shared Transformer block to recover fine-grained geometry from each prefix without a separate generative sampling stage. With the same token dimension, ZipTok3D achieves reconstruction quality comparable to the 32-token COD-VAE baseline using only one token on ShapeNet and four on TRELLIS, yielding $32\times$ and $8\times$ shorter token sequences, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Evidential Deep Learning for Multi-Modal Anti-UAV Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01742)
*Dmitry Golovchits,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验评估了证据深度学习（EDL）头部、Dempster-Shafer（DS）证据融合及不确定性驱动的时序传感器门控在反无人机（Anti-UAV）检测中的有效性。实验表明，EDL训练目标本身能显著提升精度（准确性最高提升+5.9个百分点），但其不确定性估计在检测层面失效，DS融合不优于简单概率平均，时序门控仅在近乎不激活时才能保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反无人机系统日益融合多传感器，但检测头缺乏针对各模态的可靠性信号。研究者希望探究EDL头、DS证据融合和不确定性驱动的时序传感器门控是否能改善反无人机检测性能。

Method: 在三个基准（thermal跟踪AntiUAV600、RGB-audio-RF分类TRIDENT、RGB-IR跟踪MM-UAV）上进行受控消融实验，分别验证EDL训练目标、DS证据融合、不确定性时序门控及裁剪级对照组的有效性。

Result: EDL训练目标带来显著增益（E1精度+5.9个百分点、跟踪器3倍假阳性率；E2分类精度+4.8个百分点，聚簇bootstrap p=0.011；熵UAUC约0.94 vs 0.51）。但DS融合不优于概率平均；Dirichlet空泛不确定性在分类层无额外排序能力，且在检测层因极度背景不平衡而反转失效；时序门控仅在近乎不激活时保持精度且无实际延迟节省。

Conclusion: 证据学习的收益主要来自其训练目标而非不确定性估计；检测层面的失效可定位于锚点级评估而非学习表征本身。

Abstract: Anti-UAV systems increasingly fuse multiple sensors, yet their detection heads provide no per-modality reliability signal. This study evaluates whether evidential deep learning (EDL) heads, Dempster-Shafer (DS) evidence fusion, and uncertainty-driven temporal sensor gating improve anti-UAV detection through a controlled ablation on three benchmarks: thermal tracking (AntiUAV600), RGB-audio-RF classification (TRIDENT), and RGB-IR tracking (MM-UAV). The EDL training objective improves accuracy over retrained sigmoid baselines (+5.9 percentage points in accuracy and a tripled tracker-on-absent rate in E1; +4.8 percentage points in classification accuracy in E2, surviving a clip-clustered bootstrap, p = 0.011) and ranks classification errors substantially better (entropy UAUC approximately 0.94 vs. 0.51). The remaining components do not support their respective hypotheses. DS fusion does not outperform simple probability averaging. Dirichlet vacuity adds no ranking power beyond predictive entropy and inverts at the detection level, where extreme background imbalance causes it to encode class membership rather than error likelihood, a failure also observed for entropy and sigmoid confidence. Temporal gating preserves accuracy only when nearly inactive and yields no realised latency saving on shared-backbone hardware. The benefit of evidential learning therefore arises primarily from its training objective rather than its uncertainty estimate; a crop-level control further localises the detection-level breakdown to anchor-level evaluation rather than the learned representation.

</details>


### [9] [SCULPT: Training Edge Vision Models for Post-Training Quantization Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01743)
*Bharadwaj Kavuri,Sourav Babu-PK,Varadhraj Ellapan,Pullarao Maddu,Prasad Deshpande*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT是一种训练期方法，通过在标准FP32微调过程中结合拓扑感知激活正则化（抑制量化不友好的偏度与峰度）和基于百分位数的稳定裁剪机制，改善模型对低比特后训练量化（PTQ）的适配性，无需QAT的量化模拟或运行时激活变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视觉模型难以部署在资源受限硬件上，低比特PTQ具有吸引力。但标准FP32训练常产生重尾激活分布，其中的离群值会破坏激活量化（保留全范围浪费量化bin在罕见极值上，激进裁剪又造成信息损失）。现有方案依赖QAT（增加训练复杂度和比特宽度耦合）或事后修复的PTQ方法。

Method: SCULPT包含两个核心组件：(1) 拓扑感知激活正则化器，在训练中抑制激活分布的偏度和峰度，使其更利于量化；(2) 基于百分位数的稳定裁剪机制，学习可直接部署的激活边界。训练时不模拟量化，导出的裁剪边界可直接用于标准PTQ流程。

Result: SCULPT支持INT8及更低比特设置（如W4A8）的部署，学习到的裁剪边界可直接嵌入标准PTQ工作流，无需运行时激活变换。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过在常规FP32微调阶段提升模型的PTQ就绪度，弥合了训练与量化部署之间的鸿沟，既不增加QAT的训练复杂度，也不依赖PTQ的事后修复，为低比特边缘视觉模型部署提供了一种更轻量的训练期解决方案。

Abstract: Edge vision models are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware, making low-bit post-training quantization (PTQ) attractive. In practice, standard FP32 training often produces heavy-tailed activation distributions whose outliers destabilize activation quantization: preserving the full range wastes quantization bins on rare extremes, while aggressive clipping causes information loss. Existing solutions typically rely on quantization-aware training (QAT), which adds training complexity and bit-width coupling, or advanced PTQ procedures that repair the model after training.
  We present SCULPT (Statistical Clipping and Uniform Loss for Post-Training), a training-time method that improves PTQ readiness during ordinary FP32 fine-tuning. SCULPT combines a topology-aware activation regularizer that suppresses quantization-hostile skewness and kurtosis with a stable percentile-based clipping mechanism that learns deployment-ready activation bounds. Unlike QAT, SCULPT does not simulate quantization during optimization; unlike post hoc outlier-repair PTQ methods, it does not require runtime activation transformations. The learned clipping bounds can be exported directly into a standard PTQ workflow for low-bit deployment, including INT8 and lower-bit settings such as W4A8.

</details>


### [10] [Swin Meets EfficientNet: Lightweight Architectures for GAN-Based Face Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01749)
*Sejuti Basu,Ashima Sood,Vijay Kumar,Sahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Swin-Transformer架构的GAN生成人脸图像检测方法，通过比较三种实现（紧凑型Swin Transformer、预训练的Swin-Tiny/Small及EfficientNetB0+Swin混合模型），发现混合模型在140K真实与虚假人脸数据集上达到99%的准确率和99.44%的召回率，优于纯Swin变体和CNN基线，证明层级CNN特征与移位窗口自注意力相结合的方案在检测GAN伪造人脸方面既高效又计算轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型（如GAN、扩散架构和自回归系统）生成的人脸图像几乎与真实照片无法区分，导致伪造图像检测日益困难，引发了关于身份盗窃、欺诈和虚假信息传播的严重担忧。现有检测系统依赖CNN或全局视觉Transformer各有局限：CNN擅长纹理局部特征但缺乏整体上下文理解，传统ViT虽能捕捉长距离结构却计算资源需求过高。

Method: 探索三种Swin-Transformer架构实现：(1)从零训练的紧凑型Swin Transformer；(2)基于ImageNet-1K预训练的Swin-Tiny和Swin-Small模型（适配二分类任务）；(3)将EfficientNet-B0的卷积处理与Swin Transformer后端结合的新型混合架构。所有模型在包含StyleGAN生成假脸及Flickr和DFDC真实图像的140K数据集上评估，采用平衡划分的训练/验证/测试集。

Result: EfficientNetB0+Swin混合模型在5,000张测试图像上达到99%的准确率和99.44%的召回率，优于纯Swin变体以及该数据集上此前的纯CNN基线。

Conclusion: 层级CNN特征与移位窗口自注意力的结合为检测GAN生成合成人脸提供了一种高效且计算轻量的方法，相较于纯Swin架构和纯CNN方法具有更好的检测性能。

Abstract: Modern generative models, such as GANs, diffusion architectures, and autoregressive systems, now produce facial images that are nearly indistinguishable from authentic photographs. This capability makes detecting forged images increasingly difficult, raising serious concerns about identity theft, fraud, and misinformation campaigns. Our research focuses specifically on GAN-generated synthetic faces, which underpin many face-centric deepfakes, and investigates efficient detection approaches using image analysis alone. Existing detection systems rely heavily on either convolutional neural networks (CNNs) or global vision transformers. While CNNs excel at identifying texture-based local features, they struggle with broader contextual understanding. Traditional Vision Transformer (ViT) models can capture long-range structures effectively, but demand substantial computational resources. Our work explores Swin-Transformer-based architectures across three implementations: a compact Swin Transformer trained from the ground up, ImageNet-1K pre-trained Swin-Tiny and Swin-Small models adapted for binary classification, and a novel hybrid combining EfficientNet-B0's convolutional processing with a Swin Transformer backend. We evaluated all models using the 140K Real and Fake Faces dataset, which includes StyleGAN-generated fake faces alongside authentic images from Flickr and DFDC, with balanced splits for training, validation, and testing. The EfficientNetB0+Swin hybrid achieved 99% accuracy and a 99.44% recall on 5,000 test images, outperforming both pure Swin variants and a previous CNN-only baseline on this dataset. Our results suggest that combining hierarchical CNN features with shifted-window self-attention provides an efficient and computationally lightweight method for detecting GAN-generated synthetic faces.

</details>


### [11] [AlphaRAD: Grounded Zero-Shot Classification in Chest Radiology via $α$-Corrected Binary Cross Entropy and Factorized Latent Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01757)
*Jianzhong You,Yuan Gao,Chris McIntosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaRAD，一个用于开放词汇胸部放射学理解的视觉-语言预训练框架，包含两个核心贡献：一是利用大语言模型解析胸部X光报告构建大规模结构化医学概念空间，采用α修正的二元交叉熵损失使模型成为医学概念判别器，消除了对比学习中的启发式配对匹配和批内噪声；二是提出FLaS（因子化潜在监督）跨模态融合模块，将视觉-语言表示分解为独立子空间并通过专门的联合对齐监督增强空间定位表达能力，且不引入额外模型参数。在16个分类基准上实现平均SOTA性能，并在7个定位/短语定位和3个分割数据集上取得个体SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言预训练模型在开放词汇胸部放射学理解中仍有两点未被充分探索：(1)如何从医疗报告中提取结构化临床语义以降低对比学习中的批内噪声；(2)如何设计跨模态融合机制以在不增加复杂度的前提下实现更忠实的空间定位。目前的对比学习方法依赖启发式配对匹配，且跨模态融合缺乏对空间定位表达能力的专门设计。

Method: AlphaRAD包含两大技术贡献：第一，利用LLM解析胸部X光医学报告构建大规模结构化医学概念空间用于训练，通过消除启发式配对匹配和减少批内噪声，将模型定位为医学概念判别器，使用α修正的二元交叉熵损失（α-Corrected Binary Cross-Entropy）进行优化。第二，提出FLaS（Factorized Latent Supervision）模块，将VLPM表示分解为独立子空间，用专门的联合对齐监督信号增强空间定位的判别表达能力，该模块极简且不增加额外模型参数。

Result: 通过大量实验验证，AlphaRAD在多个胸部放射学任务中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力：在16个分类基准上取得平均SOTA性能；在7个定位/短语定位数据集上以及3个分割数据集上分别实现了个体SOTA效果，展示了在分类与空间定位任务上的全面优势。

Conclusion: AlphaRAD证明利用LLM解析的结构化医学概念可以有效替代启发式配对策略并减少对比学习中的噪声；同时FLaS表明通过因子化子空间联合监督可以在不增加参数复杂度的前提下显著提升空间定位能力。该框架为开放词汇胸部放射学理解提供了新的范式，兼顾了语义判别与细粒度空间定位的双重需求。

Abstract: Vision-Language Pretrained Models (VLPMs) offer a scalable path to open-vocabulary chest radiology understanding, yet two aspects remain underexplored: how structured clinical semantics extracted from medical reports can reduce in-batch noise during contrastive learning, and how cross-modal fusion can be designed to produce more faithful spatial grounding without added complexity. We introduce AlphaRAD, addressing these opportunities through two contributions. First, we construct a large-scale structured medical concept space from medical reports parsed by a Large Language Model for training, thereby mitigating in-batch learning noise and removing heuristic pair matching in contrastive learning, and thus naturally positioning AlphaRAD as a medical concept discriminator trained via $α$-Corrected Binary Cross-Entropy. Second, we propose FLaS (Factorized Latent Supervision), an extremely simple yet effective cross-modal feature fusion module that factorizes VLPM representations into independent subspaces, using dedicated alignment supervision to enhance the expressiveness of spatial grounding without introducing additional model parameters. Through extensive empirical validation, AlphaRAD shows strong zero-shot generalization across diverse chest radiology tasks. Notably, it establishes state-of-the-art average performance across 16 classification benchmarks, while achieving individual state-of-the-art results via distinct gains on 7 grounding/phrase grounding and 3 segmentation datasets.

</details>


### [12] [Allocate Before You Embed: Adaptive Visual Input Allocation for Video Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01778)
*Song Jin,Zhongtao Jiang,Chenglei Shen,Huanxuan Liao,Haozhe Chi,Zhiwei Wang,Kun Xu,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AllocEmbed框架，通过 allocate-then-embed 策略在固定视觉输入预算下将更多帧纳入视频编码。轻量级分配器利用低成本预览为各帧分配分辨率，以保留更多对检索有利的细节；同时引入RDPO直接从检索反馈中学习分配策略。实验表明在MMEB-V2和LongRet基准上取得最优检索性能，且无需修改嵌入骨干即可集成到现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频检索需要在严格的视觉输入和推理预算下编码长且多样的视频。现有方法通常以小规模固定帧集合以原始分辨率进行采样，这限制了时间覆盖范围且忽视了帧的重要性。实证分析表明，即使在固定视觉输入预算下，扩大时间覆盖也能改善检索性能，且保持原始逐帧分辨率时增益更大，这揭示了许多现有方法在时间覆盖与空间保真度之间的权衡上存在不足。

Method: AllocEmbed采用"先分配后嵌入"的两阶段框架：(1) 一个轻量级分配器先利用低成本预览为每帧分配分辨率，在总视觉预算固定的前提下覆盖更多帧，在检索收益高的区域保留更多细节、在低价值区域减少视觉成本；(2) 提出Retrieval-Driven Policy Optimization (RDPO)，通过秩验证的相似性差距和置信度引导的效率激励，直接从检索反馈中端到端学习分配器策略。由于分配操作发生在嵌入骨干之前，无需修改嵌入模型或下游管道即可集成。

Result: 在MMEB-V2的V-QA和V-RET任务以及新提出的LongRet基准上，AllocEmbed在预算匹配的基线方法中取得了最佳的整体检索性能，并且其表现可在不同嵌入骨干之间迁移。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 时间和空间（分辨率）之间并不只是简单的取舍：在固定视觉预算下，合理地在更多帧间重新分配预算、优先保留高价值帧的空间细节，能系统性提升检索性能。AllocEmbed为大型视频检索提供了一种轻量、即插即用的预算重分配方案，其RDPO优化策略使分配器能从检索反馈中自主学习，具备良好的可迁移性与实用价值。

Abstract: Large-scale video retrieval requires embedding models to encode long and diverse videos under tight visual-input and inference budgets. Existing methods typically sample a small, fixed set of frames at their original resolution, limiting temporal coverage and ignoring frame importance. Our empirical analysis shows that expanding temporal coverage improves retrieval even under a fixed visual-input budget. Gains are larger when the original per-frame resolution is preserved, highlighting the complementary roles of temporal coverage and spatial fidelity. Motivated by this finding, we propose AllocEmbed, an allocate-then-embed framework that reallocates a fixed visual-input budget across more frames. A lightweight allocator uses low-cost previews to assign frame-wise resolutions before the embedding backbone, preserving more detail where it most benefits retrieval while reducing visual cost elsewhere. We further introduce Retrieval-Driven Policy Optimization (RDPO), which learns the allocator directly from retrieval feedback using a rank-validated similarity gap and a confidence-guided efficiency incentive. Operating entirely before the backbone, AllocEmbed integrates with existing retrieval systems without modifying the embedding model or downstream pipeline. Experiments on the MMEB-V2 V-QA and V-RET tasks and our LongRet benchmark show that AllocEmbed achieves the best overall retrieval performance among the evaluated budget-matched methods and transfers across embedding backbones. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jinsong8/AllocEmbed.

</details>


### [13] [Ten Architectures, One Error: Shared Failure Modes in Hyperspectral Classification under Spatially Disjoint Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01786)
*Ehsan Faghih,Fatemeh Ashrafi,Marguerite Moore,Zahra Saki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出高光谱图像分类中的随机像素划分会产生空间泄漏并虚增精度，提出一种将空间分离与模型感受野关联的无泄漏评估协议，并系统验证其对十种架构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像分类广泛采用单场景内随机像素划分，其中Salinas数据集是最常用的基准之一。然而随机划分会使大量测试像素紧邻训练像素，导致报告精度虚高，不公平地倾向于具有大感受野的模型。现有评估方法缺乏对空间泄漏的控制，因此需要一个更公平可靠的评估协议。

Method: 提出一种将空间分离与模型感受野相联系的leakage-free（无泄漏）评估协议。在Salinas数据集上，覆盖经典、光谱、光谱-空间、Transformer、视觉骨干网络和状态空间家族等十大类架构，系统比较随机划分与无泄漏划分下的性能差异和模型排名变化。

Result: 在无泄漏评估协议下，平均Macro-F1下降0.147，模型排名最多变动五个位次。此外，无泄漏评估限制了数据集上可测试的架构范围（因为每个划分仅在有限半径内支持patch），因此报告该半径与模型感受野对公平比较至关重要。研究还发现所有十种架构在很大程度上错误分类相同的像素，表明数据中存在所有模型均无法解决的光谱歧义。

Conclusion: 随机像素划分会显著虚增高光谱图像分类精度并扭曲模型性能比较。未来基准评估应报告空间分离半径与模型感受野的关系，同时光谱歧义的存在提示需要从数据本身出发改进高光谱分类任务的设定。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image classification still relies heavily on random pixel splits within a single scene. The Salinas dataset, randomly split, is among the most widely used datasets for comparing different architectures. However, under a random split method, a large fraction of test pixels fall immediately adjacent to a training pixel, which inflates reported accuracy. This work introduces a leakage-free evaluation protocol linking spatial separation to the model's receptive field. Applying this protocol across ten different architectures, including classical, spectral, spectral-spatial, transformer, vision-backbone, and state-space families, shows that Macro-F1 drops by 0.147 on average and model rankings change by as many as five places. Furthermore, leakage-free evaluation limits which architectures can be tested on a given benchmark. Since each partition supports patches only within a finite radius, reporting this radius alongside the receptive field is essential for fair comparison. In addition, this study reveals that all ten architectures misclassify largely the same pixels, pointing to a spectral ambiguity in the data that none of them resolves.

</details>


### [14] [CoViT: Instance-Correspondence Contrastive Learning for Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01787)
*Yisen Wang,Zhirong Wu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoViT，一种通过几何引导对比学习将实例感知注入Vision Transformer的自监督框架，利用注意力引导掩码和最难对比挖掘构建三元组，使纯ViT在无额外解码器或标签的情况下，在多个实例级任务上稳定提升超过2个AP点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在语义理解上表现出色，但无法区分不同对象实例（如两只狗产生相同嵌入），这限制了其在目标检测和实例分割等实例级任务中的应用。

Method: CoViT由两部分组成：(1) 注意力引导掩码：通过自适应阈值化和形态学操作细化多头注意力图，生成实例掩码并识别前景锚点；(2) 最难对比挖掘：对每个锚点计算成对嵌入相似度，选取实例内最难正样本（掩码内相似度最低的patch）和实例间最难负样本（来自其他实例的最相似patch），并在负样本搜索期间屏蔽实例内区域。这些三元组驱动对比损失，同时压缩实例内方差并扩大实例间边界。

Result: CoViT在使用ViT作为骨干架构时，在多个实例级感知任务上稳定获得超过2个AP点的性能提升，且无需任何额外解码器或标签。

Conclusion: 证明了纯ViT可以通过固有的注意力先验和定向对比约束学习实例感知的表示，为ViT在实例级任务中的应用提供了无需解耦架构修改的新路径。代码和模型将公开发布。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViT) excel in semantic understanding but fail to discriminate between object instances (e.g., identical embeddings for two dogs), limiting their use in instance-level tasks such as object detection and instance segmentation. We propose Contrastive Vision Transformer (CoViT), a self-supervised learning framework that injects instance-awareness into ViT through geometry-guided contrastive learning. CoViT uniquely coordinates ViT's attention maps and embeddings by constructing triplets: (1) Attention-guided masking: Refine multi-head attention via adaptive thresholding and morphological operations to generate instance masks, identifying foreground anchors; (2) Hardest contrastive mining: For each anchor, computing pairwise embedding similarities to select the intra-instance hardest positive (least similar patch within its mask) and inter-instance hardest negative (most similar patch from other instances), with intra-instance regions masked during negative search. These triplets drive a contrastive loss that simultaneously compresses intra-instance variance and expands inter-instance margins, forcing ViT to discern subtle geometric and appearance differences between instances. CoViT consistently achieves stable performance gains of over 2 AP points across multiple instance-level perception tasks by using ViT as backbone architecture. Notably, CoViT requires no extra decoders or labels, demonstrating that a pure ViT can learn instance-aware representations via inherent attention priors and targeted contrastive constraints. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [15] [DESA-TTA: Dynamic EMA and Source Anchoring for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01795)
*Atif Belal,Lilian Hollard,Marco Pedersoli,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DESA-TTA方法，通过动态EMA系数和源锚定机制共同应对平均教师TTA中教师更新敏感性和学生模型漂移问题，在多种分布偏移场景下显著提升VLOD的测试时自适应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言目标检测器（VLODs）在零样本性能上表现强劲，但部署时易受分布偏移影响。平均教师方法的测试时自适应（TTA）虽可提升鲁棒性，但固定EMA系数选择敏感，且用噪声伪标签反复优化会导致学生模型累积漂移。

Method: 提出DESA-TTA，包含两个核心机制：(1) 动态时序平均（Dynamic temporal averaging）：根据伪标签置信度和框密度估计教师不确定性，在由教师参数漂移决定的边界内选择样本级EMA系数；(2) 源锚定（Source anchoring）：基于学生漂移程度按强度将更新后的学生参数部分恢复到预训练值。

Result: 在多种分布偏移和两种VLOD架构上均优于现有TTA方法。在VOC-C上，DESA-TTA相较零样本推理将AP50提升14.5个百分点；相对于YOLO-World上的先前最优TTA方法，推理吞吐量提高55%。

Conclusion: DESA-TTA通过动态EMA和源锚定的联合调控，有效解决了平均教师TTA中教师更新敏感性与学生漂移问题，实现了低开销、高性能且具有更高推理效率的VLOD分布偏移自适应。

Abstract: Vision-language object detectors (VLODs) achieve strong zero-shot performance but remain vulnerable to distribution shifts during deployment. Mean-teacher methods for test-time adaptation (TTA) can improve robustness by updating a student model using teacher-generated pseudo-labels. However, mean-teacher TTA is highly sensitive to the choice of a fixed exponential moving average (EMA) coefficient for teacher updates, and repeated optimization with noisy pseudo-labels can cause cumulative student drift. We propose Dynamic EMA and Source Anchoring for TTA (DESA-TTA), a low-overhead method that jointly regulates teacher updates and student drift through dynamic temporal averaging and source anchoring. Dynamic temporal averaging estimates teacher uncertainty from pseudo-label confidence and box density and uses it to select a sample-wise EMA coefficient within bounds determined by teacher parameter drift. Source anchoring partially restores the updated student parameters toward their pretrained values, with the anchoring strength increasing according to student drift. Experiments across diverse distribution shifts and two VLOD architectures show consistent improvements over existing TTA methods. On VOC-C, DESA-TTA improves AP$_{50}$ by 14.5 points over zero-shot inference while achieving 55\% higher inference throughput than the previous state-of-the-art TTA method for YOLO-World. Our code: https://github.com/imatif17/DESA-TTA

</details>


### [16] [Improved Automatic Target Recognition in Synthetic Aperture Sonar Imagery Using Large Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01800)
*C. J. Moore,Alex Hurt,Jordan Malof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了CNN与Transformer架构在合成孔径声纳自动目标识别（SAS-ATR）中的性能，并研究了网络规模、预训练方法、数据增强和正则化策略对模型效果的影响，旨在找出最优模型并给出训练SAS-ATR模型的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAS-ATR领域主要由CNN架构主导，但Transformer在通用计算机视觉中已是SOTA，在SAS-ATR中研究不足；同时，由于标记训练数据稀缺，数据增强和使用不同模态预训练权重等方法的效果参差不齐，缺乏系统性比较。

Method: 对比现代CNN与基于Transformer的深度神经网络（DNN）在SAS-ATR任务中的表现，系统考察网络规模、架构类型、预训练方法、数据增强及其他正则化手段各因素对性能的影响。

Result: 通过系统性的消融与比较实验，确定了在SAS-ATR中产生最高性能的架构与训练配置组合，并总结出训练SOTA级SAS-ATR模型的实践指南。

Conclusion: 提供了针对SAS-ATR任务选择最优深度学习架构与训练策略的路线图，填补了Transformer在此领域应用研究的空白，为该领域后续研究提供了可参考的训练范式。

Abstract: Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) in Synthetic Aperture Sonar (SAS) is a task largely dominated by deep neural networks (DNNs). Most SAS-ATR models use convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures whereas transformer-based architectures have had much less representation in the literature despite being state of the art in general computer vision (CV) research. Additionally, researchers have had mixed results in attempting to overcome challenges presented by a scarcity of labeled training data by using methods such as data augmentation and the use of pretrained weights from a variety of imaging modalities. In this work, we compare the performance of modern CNN and transformer-based DNNs to determine which architecture and training configurations elicit the highest performance in SAS-ATR. We investigate how network size, architecture, pretraining method, data augmentation and other forms of regularization affect SAS-ATR performance with a focus on producing the highest-performing model and providing a roadmap for training state-of-the-art SAS-ATR models.

</details>


### [17] [Consistency as Regularization for Unsupervised Shadow Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01806)
*Anh-Kiet Duong,Petra Gomez-Krämer,Jean-Michel Carozza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShadowCLR是一种无需阴影掩码或阴影图像参考的无监督阴影去除框架，仅从阴影图像中学习，通过利用阴影观测间的一致性作为正则化，恢复场景一致外观同时抑制阴影特定变化，在多个基准上达到与最先进无监督方法竞争甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督阴影去除方法需要成对的阴影与无阴影图像，无监督方法也往往依赖阴影掩码或无阴影参考图像，这些数据获取成本高且难以大规模可用。作者旨在完全摆脱这些监督信号，仅从阴影图像本身学习阴影去除。

Method: 关键洞察是阴影在不同观测中变化，而底层场景内容保持大体一致。因此，ShadowCLR将阴影观测间的一致性作为正则化，鼓励模型恢复场景一致外观并抑制阴影特定变化。通过全局和局部一致性，该方法能够利用视觉相关的图像、从不完美对齐的观测中学习，并聚焦于共享场景信息的表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，ShadowCLR在无需阴影掩码或无阴影图像的情况下，取得了与最先进无监督方法相比竞争性甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: 一致性约束可以作为阴影去除的有效正则化手段，证明了在完全没有阴影掩码或无阴影参考图像的条件下实现阴影去除是可行的。

Abstract: Shadow removal is an important preprocessing step for many vision tasks, yet existing supervised methods require paired shadow and shadow-free images, while unsupervised approaches often still rely on shadow masks or shadow-free references. We propose ShadowCLR, an unsupervised framework that learns shadow removal directly from shadow images. Our key observation is that shadows vary across observations while the underlying scene content remains largely consistent. We therefore use consistency across shadow observations as regularization, encouraging the model to recover scene-consistent appearance while suppressing shadow-specific variations. Global and local consistency further enable us to explore visually related images, learn from imperfectly aligned observations, and focus the representation on shared scene information. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ShadowCLR achieves competitive and often superior performance over state-of-the-art unsupervised methods, demonstrating that consistency can provide regularization for shadow removal without shadow masks or shadow-free images.

</details>


### [18] [Integrated Laser Scanning and Image-Based Topology Optimization Techniques for Detection and Quantification of Visible and Subsurface Structural Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01808)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Devin Harris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补的非接触式视觉检测方法用于结构构件缺陷的定性与定量表征：一种基于高分辨率激光扫描的3D点云技术定位表面损伤并量化几何损失，另一种结合3D-DIC全场变形测量与有限元模型更新/拓扑优化来推断次表面异常。实验验证表明两种方法均可有效识别缺陷，并为可见与不可见损伤评估提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的结构缺陷表征需要同时解析直接可见的表面损伤和从检测表面无法观测到的内部/次表面损伤，现有单一检测手段难以兼顾两者，因此需要开发互补性的方法体系。

Method: 方法一：高分辨率激光扫描生成3D点云，通过测量点云与参考点云的比较处理定位损伤区域、量化几何损失，并将缺陷几何传递至有限元模型。方法二：利用3D-DIC获取全场表面变形，结合有限元模型更新和拓扑优化的逆问题框架，根据表面响应空间分布推断次表面异常。实验采用含受控光滑缺陷和随机分布缺陷的钢梁试件进行验证。

Result: 与基于铣削加工的地面真值测量对比表明，两种方法均能识别并量化缺陷几何；两种方法分别针对表面可见损伤与次表面不可见损伤提供互补的检测能力，准确性与可靠性得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 该系统性的组合框架为具有复杂和不规则损伤的结构构件建立了高保真、非接触式结构状态评估与模型更新的可行路径。

Abstract: Reliable characterization of structural defects requires methods capable of resolving both directly observable surface damage and damage that is not visible from the inspected surface. This study presents two complementary non-contact, vision-based approaches for the detection and quantitative characterization of defects in structural components. The first approach employs high-resolution laser scanning to generate three-dimensional (3D) point clouds of damaged steel specimens. Comparative processing of measured and reference point clouds is used to localize damaged regions, quantify geometric loss, and transfer the measured defect geometry to a finite element representation. The second approach combines full-field surface deformation measurements obtained using three-dimensional digital image correlation (3D-DIC) with finite element model updating and topology optimization. In this inverse framework, measured surface response is used to infer subsurface abnormalities through their influence on the spatial distribution of structural response. Experimental steel-beam specimens containing controlled smooth defects and randomly distributed defects are used to evaluate the approaches. Comparisons with milling-based ground-truth measurements demonstrate that both methods can identify and quantify defect geometry, while providing complementary information for visible and subsurface damage assessment. The combined framework establishes a pathway toward high-fidelity, non-contact structural condition assessment and model updating for components with complex and irregular damage.

</details>


### [19] [Video2Reaction: Training Foundation Video Models to Predict Audience Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01816)
*Sidong Zhang,Trang Nguyen,Shiv Shankar,Gauri Jagatap,Deepak Chandran,Andrea Fanelli,Madalina Fiterau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video2Reaction，一个将短视频片段映射到观众在社交媒体评论中表达的情感反应的多模态数据集。该数据集通过聚合大规模在线评论来建模情感标签的分布，以反映情感感知的主观性和模糊性。作者使用LoRA微调两种视觉语言模型（VLMs）进行基准测试，证明其优于专门基线；并展示VLMs在Video2Reaction上预微调后能有效迁移到VCE数据集，其中LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B仅用VCE 1%训练数据即可达到与全量训练相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的诱导情感（induced emotion）数据集通常依赖实验室环境或小规模标注，难以捕捉真实世界中观众情感反应的多样性和主观性。作者旨在构建一个大规模的野外面向数据资源，利用社交媒体评论作为情感信号的天然来源，更好地建模情感感知的模糊本质。

Method: 1) 构建Video2Reaction数据集：从社交媒体在线评论中大规模聚合观众对短视频片段的情绪反应，将情感标签建模为分类分布而非单标签；2) 使用LoRA微调两种视觉语言模型（VLMs）用于主导情感反应预测；3) 通过在不同分类体系和视频领域的VCE数据集上进行预微调+迁移实验评估泛化能力。

Result: 微调后的VLMs在Video2Reaction数据集上优于专门的情感反应预测基线。在跨数据集迁移中，LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B经过Video2Reaction预微调后在VCE 1%训练数据上取得0.682的top-3准确率，与在全量VCE数据上训练得到的最佳报告性能相当。

Conclusion: Video2Reaction是一个有效的野外面向情感反应数据集，能以分布标签形式捕捉情感的主观多样性。在该数据集上预微调的VLMs展现出色性能和跨数据集迁移能力，表明大规模社交媒体评论可以作为视频诱发情感研究的丰富数据源，并以数据高效的方式适应目标领域。

Abstract: We introduce Video2Reaction, a multimodal dataset that maps short movie segments to the induced emotional reactions of viewers in the wild, as expressed through social media comments. Video2Reaction captures the natural diversity of emotional responses by aggregating reactions from online comments at scale, modeling labels as distributions over categorical emotions to better reflect the subjective and ambiguous nature of emotional perception. We benchmark two vision-language models (VLMs) finetuned with LoRA, showing that VLMs learn effectively from Video2Reaction and outperform specialized baselines on dominant reaction prediction. We further demonstrate that VLMs pre-finetuned on Video2Reaction transfer effectively to VCE, another induced emotion dataset with a different taxonomy and video domain. Notably, LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B pre-finetuned on Video2Reaction and adapted on only 1% of VCE training data achieves a top-3 accuracy of 0.682, on par with the best reported VCE performance trained on the full dataset. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/infofusionlab/Video2Reaction

</details>


### [20] [Kirin: Animal Motion Generation from In-the-Wild Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01823)
*Brian Nlong Zhao,Zhuoyang Pan,James M. Rehg,Jiajun Wu,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Kirin框架，通过从自然视频中重建动物3D运动序列并配以文本字幕，构建了首个大规模的四足动物视频-文本-运动对齐数据集AiM3D，并在此基础上训练视觉引导的运动生成模型，最终结合图像转3D模型实现自动绑定与动画化，为大规模文本/图像条件驱动的动物运动生成与动画提供了新基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物运动理解对动物行为与生物力学建模至关重要，但进展远落后于人体运动研究，主要原因是缺乏高质量运动数据。由于多数动物无法在受控环境中进行运动捕捉，现有数据集规模小且领域受限，限制了如动画等下游应用。

Method: 1) 利用大规模自然场景动物视频，重建3D运动序列并生成文本字幕，构建AiM3D数据集；2) 基于该数据集训练视觉引导的运动生成模型，以文本和图像为条件生成多样物种的真实运动；3) 利用现成的图像转3D模型，对3D网格进行自动绑定并施加生成的运动，实现可渲染的动画化动物。

Result: 构建了首个大规模四足动物的视频-文本-运动对齐数据集AiM3D，并开发了支持文本与图像双重条件驱动的动物运动生成与完整动画化流水线。

Conclusion: Kirin数据集与框架为大规模、文本和图像条件下的动物运动生成与动画建立了新基础，弥合了动物运动研究与人/动物运动研究之间的数据差距。

Abstract: Understanding animal motion is fundamental to modeling animal behavior and biomechanics, yet progress in this area lags far behind human motion research due to the scarcity of high-quality motion data. While human motion can be captured in controlled environments, it is impractical for most animal species, resulting in small, domain-limited datasets that restrict downstream applications such as animation. To address this challenge, we introduce Kirin, a framework that reconstructs motion from video, learns motion priors at scale, and generates realistic motion that can be directly applied to animated assets. Using large collections of in-the-wild animal videos, we reconstruct 3D motion sequences and pair them with captions to create AiM3D, the first large-scale dataset offering aligned video-text-motion tuples for quadruped animals. Building on this dataset, we develop a visual-guided motion generation model that conditions on both text and image to guide the generation of realistic motion across diverse animal species. Finally, by leveraging an off-the-shelf image-to-3D model, we automatically rig and animate 3D meshes using generated motion, producing ready-to-render animated animals. Together, our dataset and framework establish a new foundation for large-scale, text and image conditioned animal motion generation and animation. Project page: https://kirin-ani.github.io/.

</details>


### [21] [SliceBridge: context-consistent repair of corrupted slice intervals in T1-weighted MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01827)
*Jiheng Li,Michael E. Kim,Trent Schwartz,Gaurav Rudravaram,Derek B. Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,Lianrui Zuo,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SliceBridge框架，用于修复T1加权MRI中局部连续切片损坏的问题。该方法使用基于rectified flow matching的条件生成模型，以受损区间周围的完好切片及其相对位置为条件，重建受损切片区间。通过区间相关初始噪声、共享流动时间和同步采样来促进跨平面一致性，将修复的区间插回原图像。模型在9,877个训练体数据和4个数据集上进行训练验证，并在581个外部受试者上评估，结果显示在切片间变化误差上降低了32.9%-41.3%，SSIM指标在所有区间长度上均更高；在受控损坏场景下，下游分割模型产生的区域脑容量估计中位误差从1.95%降至1.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构性MRI图像常出现连续切片区域的局部损坏（如采集、运动、硬件或重建效应导致），这种局部损坏会干扰下游形态测量分析。丢弃或重采整个图像成本高昂，因此需要一种能够利用周围解剖和成像上下文来重建损坏区间的图像修复方法。

Method: 提出SliceBridge框架：将损坏区间修复建模为基于rectified flow matching的图像修复问题。模型以受损区间周围完好的切片及其相对切片位置为条件进行生成。通过区间相关的初始噪声、共享流动时间和同步采样来增强切片间的跨平面一致性。修复完成后将重建区间插回原图像，不改变其他切片。

Result: 在9,877个T1加权脑MRI体数据（来自4个数据集）上训练，在581个外部受试者上评估。SliceBridge在修复区间内切片间变化误差上比独立重建基线模型降低32.9%-41.3%，并在所有区间长度上取得更高SSIM。在受控损坏情况下，将下游分割模型产生的区域脑容量估计中位误差从1.95%降至1.05%。

Conclusion: SliceBridge通过利用周围上下文的生成式修复方法，能有效恢复MRI中局部连续的切片损坏，显著改善修复区间内的跨平面一致性，并减轻下游形态测量分析中的偏差，为受损但其他部分尚可用的图像提供了低成本修复方案。

Abstract: Structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) images are sometimes corrupted over a contiguous set of slices, where acquisition, motion, hardware, or reconstruction effects leave a single slice or short interval inconsistent with its neighbors while the rest of the image remains usable. Such localized corruption can bias downstream morphometric analysis, yet discarding or reacquiring an otherwise usable image is costly. We formulate this as an image restoration problem: given the location of the affected interval, reconstruct those slices from the surrounding anatomical and imaging context. We propose SliceBridge, a framework for restoring corrupted slice intervals in T1-weighted MRI using rectified flow matching conditioned on the surrounding intact slices and their relative slice positions. Through-plane consistency is encouraged by coupling the slices within the interval through interval-correlated initial noise, a shared flow time, and synchronized sampling. The restored interval is then inserted back, leaving all other slices unchanged. We trained and validated the model on 9,877 T1-weighted brain MRI volumes from four datasets and evaluated it on 581 external subjects using clean interval withholding and controlled corruptions. Compared with a matched model that reconstructed target slices independently, SliceBridge reduced error in slice-to-slice changes within repaired intervals by 32.9%-41.3% across interval lengths and achieved higher SSIM at every interval length. In controlled-corruption cases, SliceBridge reduced the median error in regional brain volume estimates produced by a downstream segmentation model from 1.95% in corrupted volumes to 1.05%.

</details>


### [22] [Cross-Model Distillation of a Human-Pose Foundation Model from Unannotated Infant Video for Markerless 3D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01840)
*R. James Cotton,Divya Joshi,Colleen Peyton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过跨模型蒸馏方法，将Sapiens 2姿态模型的2D关键点知识蒸馏到SAM 3D Body模型中，仅使用无标注的婴儿视频即可提升婴儿自发运动的3D姿态估计精度，克服了基础模型主要基于成人数据训练导致的婴儿适用性不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿自发运动是神经运动健康的重要早期窗口，但现有临床评估工具需要专业评分员、耗时且存在评分者间差异。自动化的基于视频无标记姿态评估是理想方案，然而支撑无标记运动捕捉的基础模型几乎全部基于成人数据训练，此前多视角婴儿研究发现单一模型无法同时达到最优的2D关键点精度和3D身体重建效果，存在trade-off而未被解决。

Method: 采用跨模型蒸馏（cross-model distillation）策略：将Sapiens 2姿态模型的强2D关键点能力蒸馏到SAM 3D Body模型中。具体地，冻结的教师模型（Sapiens）提供密集伪标签，可微渲染器在训练循环中将SAM 3D Body预测的网格与伪标签对齐，仅使用无标注的婴儿视频进行微调训练。

Result: 在11名留出婴儿（18个session，173段录像）的多视角协议下，微调后同视角2D关键点与Sapiens参考的一致性显著提升（身体PCK@10px从0.22提升至0.42，面部从0.22提升至0.42），Procrustes对齐的平均每关节3D位置误差从25.5mm降至22.2mm。

Conclusion: 跨模型蒸馏能够有效提升SAM 3D Body模型在婴儿上的表现，证明了在不同基础模型之间整合互补优势（强2D关键点与直接3D身体恢复）的可行性，为婴儿无标记运动评估提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Spontaneous movement is one of the earliest windows onto an infant's neuromotor health, and structured clinical instruments that score it are validated early predictors of cerebral-palsy risk. However, they require specially trained raters, are time-consuming, and carry inter-rater variability. This motivates automated, video-based markerless assessment, especially as marker-based motion capture is impractical in infants. Yet the foundation models that make markerless capture possible are trained almost entirely on adults: our recent multi-view infant study found that no single model is jointly best, with strong 2D keypoint accuracy and direct 3D body recovery split across different models. While that study identifies this trade-off, it does not resolve it. Here, we perform cross-model distillation from the Sapiens 2 pose model into the SAM 3D Body model, using unannotated infant video alone. A frozen teacher supplies dense pseudo-labels, and a differentiable renderer aligns the predicted mesh to them in the training loop. On eleven held-out infants (18 sessions, 173 recordings) under our prior study's multi-view protocol, fine-tuning improves same-view 2D keypoint agreement with the Sapiens reference (median body percentage of correct keypoints @ 10px 0.22 -> 0.42, face 0.22 -> 0.42) and Procrustes-aligned mean per joint 3D position error (25.5 -> 22.2 mm). This demonstrates how cross-model distillation improves SAM 3D Body model performance on infants.

</details>


### [23] [RAFT-DVC: Resolution-Aware Machine Learning-Based Digital Volume Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01876)
*Zixiang Tong,Lehu Bu,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAFT-DVC，一个基于RAFT架构、具有分辨率感知能力（编码器下采样因子s=2,4,8）的系列数字体相关（DVC）求解器。三个求解器将位移定位到约0.017特征网格体素，经验原始体积误差缩放约为0.017s体素，显示出由位移范围和体纹理兼容性共同决定的互补工作区间。在细纹理、小到中等位移条件下与调优的经典DVC精度相当，在粗纹理、大位移条件下具备竞争优势，并通过跨纹理迁移验证其泛化能力。同时识别并修正了三维RAFT相关采样中的坐标顺序不一致问题，引入非立方脉冲测试验证采样器几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DVC技术从体积图像中提供三维全场位移测量，但基于机器学习（具体为RAFT）的DVC模型内部分辨率如何影响精度和操作范围尚未被充分理解。作者旨在系统研究编码器下采样因子对DVC求解器性能的影响，并提供一个分辨率感知的框架。

Method: 基于RAFT架构构建三个分辨率不同的DVC求解器（编码器下采样因子s=2,4,8），采用匹配设计进行系统比较；通过合成基准测试评估精度，频率扫描测试量化变形空间分辨率，分块推理（tiled inference）支持大体积稠密估计；提出非立方脉冲测试以独立验证采样器几何结构，并修正三维RAFT相关采样中的坐标顺序不一致问题。

Result: 三个求解器定位位移约0.017特征网格体素，原始体积误差约0.017s体素。合成基准显示RAFT-DVC在细纹理小位移条件下与调优经典DVC精度同量级，在粗纹理大位移条件下具有竞争力或优势。在压痕共聚焦图像和弹性泡沫微CT图像上验证了操作区间匹配和跨纹理迁移能力。修正采样器坐标顺序问题后提升了原生输入精度和尺寸泛化能力。

Conclusion: RAFT-DVC作为一个快速、分辨率感知的稠密DVC框架被确立，其精度和工作区间已得到系统表征。校准采样器几何结构对精度提升和泛化至关重要，该框架为不同变形尺度和纹理条件下选择适当分辨率的DVC求解器提供了指导。

Abstract: Digital volume correlation (DVC) provides three-dimensional full-field displacement measurements from volumetric images, but how the internal resolution of a machine-learning-based DVC model affects accuracy and operating range remains poorly understood. Here, we present RAFT-DVC, a resolution-aware family of recurrent all-pairs field transforms (RAFT)-based DVC solvers with encoder downsampling factors s = 2, 4, and 8. Using a matched design, we find that the three solvers localize displacement to approximately 0.017 feature-grid voxel, giving an empirical raw-volume error scaling of approximately 0.017s voxel. The solvers exhibit complementary operating regimes governed jointly by displacement reach and volumetric-texture compatibility. Synthetic benchmarks show that RAFT-DVC achieves errors of the same order as tuned classical DVC under fine-texture, small-to-moderate-displacement conditions and becomes competitive or advantageous under coarse-texture, large-displacement conditions. Frequency-swept tests quantify deformation spatial resolution, while tiled inference enables dense estimation on large volumes. Evaluation on confocal volumetric images acquired during indentation illustrates the importance of matching solver operating regime to deformation magnitude and image texture. Tests on micro-CT images of elastomeric foam, despite training only on particle-labeled synthetic data, provide evidence of cross-texture transfer. We also identify coordinate-order inconsistencies in three-dimensional RAFT correlation sampling and introduce a non-cubic impulse test to verify sampler geometry independently of network training. Correcting the sampler improves native-input accuracy and generalization to unseen volume dimensions. Together, these results establish RAFT-DVC as a fast, resolution-aware framework for dense DVC with characterized accuracy and operating regimes.

</details>


### [24] [SignMatch: Matching Dictionary Signs to Continuous Sign Language Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01886)
*Ryan Wong,Youngjoon Jang,Liliane Momeni,Gül Varol,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种原型结构的符号嵌入空间，将孤立词典视频与连续手语视频中的对应符号进行匹配（仅依据视觉相似性：手形及相对身体的运动）。通过学习带符号标注的连续视频，每个可学习原型对应一个符号类别，然后将孤立词典视频映射到该空间实现匹配。该方法可直接通过嵌入相似度进行词典引导的符号匹配，并可仅凭词典示例自然扩展到未见符号。在ASL-Citizen、ChaLearn OSLWL和BOBSL三个基准上跨数据集、任务及美/英/西班牙手语均表现优异，超越先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语理解中一个核心挑战是词典中的孤立符号视频与连续手语视频中自然出现的对应符号之间存在严重的分布与形态差异。现有方法通常需要针对特定基准进行专门监督，难以跨数据集、跨手语泛化。本文动机在于构建一个无需基准特定监督、仅依赖视觉相似性（手形与相对身体运动）即可实现词典到连续手语符号匹配的通用框架。

Method: 论文提出从带符号标注的连续视频中学习一个"原型结构化"的符号嵌入空间：每个可学习的原型向量对应当前数据集中的一个符号类别。训练后，将孤立词典视频通过同一编码器映射到该共享嵌入空间，使得词典示例与连续视频中的符号实例在该空间中可直接通过嵌入相似度进行匹配。此外，该设计支持词典引导的匹配范式，可借助词典示例对未见符号进行零样本/少样本扩展，不需要基准特定的微调监督。

Result: 在三个不同基准（ASL-Citizen词典检索、ChaLearn OSLWL词典到连续符号匹配，以及基于BOBSL CSLR2评估的自动符号标注）上进行实验，表明该方法在多种数据集、任务和手语（美国、英国、西班牙手语）上均展现出强泛化能力。在无基准特定监督的条件下，所学表示能够有效迁移，并在全部三个基准上优于此前方法。

Conclusion: 通过原型结构的符号嵌入空间，本文证明了一个无需基准特定监督的表示学习方案可以在词典-连续手语符号匹配任务上实现跨语言、跨数据集的强泛化。这一方法为词典引导的自动手语标注与检索提供了通用且可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to match dictionary sign videos to corresponding signs in continuous signing videos, where a match is defined by the visual similarity alone - the handshape and motion relative to the body. To achieve this, we learn a prototype-structured sign embedding space from continuous video annotated with signs, where each learnable prototype corresponds to a sign class. Isolated dictionary videos are then mapped into this sign space, enabling the matching between dictionary exemplars and continuous sign instances. This design supports direct dictionary-guided sign matching through embedding similarity and naturally extends to unseen signs using only dictionary exemplars. Experiments on ASL-Citizen dictionary retrieval, ChaLearn OSLWL dictionary-to-continuous sign matching, and using BOBSL's CSLR2 evaluation for automatic sign annotation demonstrate strong generalisation across datasets, tasks and sign languages. Without benchmark-specific supervision, the learned representation transfers effectively across American, British, and Spanish Sign Languages, outperforming prior methods on all three benchmarks. Project page: https://www.robots.ox.ac.uk/~vgg/research/signmatch/

</details>


### [25] [Does Playing it Safe Count as Faithfulness? Reassessing LVLM Hallucination Mitigation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01888)
*Mehrdad Fazli,Sina Mansouri,Mohit Marvania,Ziwei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种LVLM推理时幻觉缓解方法在三个模型和四个基准上的表现，发现幻觉分数的降低往往伴随着信息量的减少，且对更广泛的多模态能力无可靠迁移，主张将幻觉缓解视为"忠实性-信息量-能力"的权衡而非仅看幻觉分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉缓解方法在基准上报告了显著的改进，但研究者质疑这些改进是否真正反映了多模态基础能力的提升，还是仅仅源于模型生成更保守（即避免犯错但内容贫乏）的回答。需要对这一关键混淆进行系统分析。

Method: 作者在三个LVLM和四个基准（包括专门评估幻觉的基准和综合能力基准MMStar）上评估了六种推论阶段幻觉缓解方法，同时考察幻觉分数、物体召回率、视觉覆盖率和回答详细程度等维度，以区分"更好的多模态基础"与"更保守的生成"。

Result: 发现两个一致模式：(1) 幻觉降低常常伴随信息量减少——降低幻觉率的方法同时降低了物体召回、视觉覆盖率或回答详细度；(2) 幻觉基准上的改进不能可靠迁移到更广泛的多模态能力，方法在细粒度感知和推理任务上表现不一致甚至退化。

Conclusion: 当前评估协议可能通过奖励保守生成而高估了进步。幻觉缓解应作为忠实性-信息量-能力三者之间的权衡来评估，而非仅通过幻觉分数单独衡量。

Abstract: Recent inference-time hallucination mitigation methods for large vision-language models (LVLMs) report strong gains on hallucination benchmarks. However, it remains unclear whether lower hallucination scores reflect improved multimodal grounding or more conservative generation. We evaluate six mitigation methods across three LVLMs and four benchmarks, including hallucination-focused evaluation and the diverse capability benchmark MMStar. Our analysis reveals two consistent patterns. First, hallucination reduction is often coupled with reduced informativeness: methods that lower hallucination rates also reduce object recall, visual coverage, or response detailedness. Second, improvements on hallucination benchmarks do not reliably transfer to broader multimodal capabilities, with methods showing inconsistent or degraded performance on fine-grained perception and reasoning tasks. Our findings suggest that current evaluation protocols may overestimate progress by rewarding conservative generation. We argue that hallucination mitigation should be evaluated as a faithfulness--informativeness--capability trade-off rather than through hallucination scores alone.

</details>


### [26] [TAPVid-MV: A Benchmark for Tracking Any Point in 3D Across Multiple Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01899)
*Skanda Koppula,Frano Rajic,Abdullah Faiz Ur Rahman,Yi Yang,Ignacio Rocco,Jeet Thakwani,Rishabh Kabra,Andrew Zisserman,Joao Carreira,Siyu Tang,Carl Doersch,Gabriel Brostow*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAPVid-MV是首个跨多个同步视角、相机运动下的长时3D点跟踪基准，包含284个序列、1,142个标定相机流和109,769个点轨迹，涵盖七个子集。评估超过30个基线后发现现有方法均未解决该任务，且多视角点跟踪器并未稳定优于单目点跟踪器，分析表明几何恢复是3D点跟踪精度的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相机系统在机器人、AR/VR和自动驾驶中日益重要，互补视角可减少深度模糊并在遮挡下保持可见性。然而现有点跟踪基准仅关注单一视频或静态多相机阵列，缺乏在相机运动条件下跨多个同步视角的长时3D点跟踪基准，无法验证和推动该方向的方法发展。

Method: 构建TAPVid-MV基准：收集284个序列（1,142个标定相机流）横跨七个子集，覆盖室内外、机器人、人体活动、驾驶和合成场景；利用数据集特定的辅助模态（传感器深度、LiDAR、SLAM/SfM点、人体网格、物体网格、仿真）获取轨迹，并由人工标注者逐序列逐轨迹视觉验证。同时联合评估重建与点跟踪以区分几何误差和对应误差。

Result: 评估超过30个基线，无方法接近解决该任务；现有多视角点跟踪器未稳定超越单目点跟踪器；通过联合分析发现几何恢复是3D点跟踪准确性的主要瓶颈。发布的标注还支持单目2D/3D点跟踪、未来轨迹预测和4D重建。

Conclusion: TAPVid-MV填补了多视角3D点跟踪基准的空白，揭示了该任务仍未解决，并指出几何恢复是关键瓶颈，为未来研究提供了明确方向和丰富的数据资源。

Abstract: Multi-camera systems are increasingly practical for robotics, AR/VR, and autonomous driving because complementary views reduce depth ambiguity and preserve visibility under occlusion. Existing point-tracking benchmarks, however, focus on a single video or static multi-camera rigs. None test long-term 3D point tracking across several synchronized views under camera motion. We introduce TAPVid-MV (Tracking Any Point in Video across Multiple Views), the first benchmark for this setting. It contains a curated set of 284 sequences, 1,142 calibrated camera streams, and 109,769 point tracks across seven subsets spanning indoor and outdoor domains, from robotics and human activity to driving and synthetic procedural scenes. We obtain these trajectories using dataset-specific auxiliary modalities: sensor depth, LiDAR, SLAM and SfM points, human meshes, posed object meshes, and simulation. Every sequence and trajectory is visually verified by human annotators.
  Across more than 30 baselines, no method comes close to solving the task. Surprisingly, existing multi-view point trackers do not consistently outperform monocular point trackers. By evaluating reconstruction and point tracking on the same datasets, TAPVid-MV helps distinguish errors in recovered geometry from errors in point correspondence. Through this joint analysis, we identify geometry recovery as a major bottleneck for accurate 3D point tracking. Beyond multi-view 3D point tracking, our released annotations support monocular 2D and 3D point tracking, future-trajectory prediction, and 4D reconstruction.

</details>


### [27] [Automated Maize Ear Phenotyping Using 3D Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01921)
*Ritwesh A. Kumar,Som Tripathi,Peja Matthews,Srikar Reddy,Talukder Zaki Jubery,Patrick Schnable,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一个全自动流水线，用于从玉米果穗的3D点云中提取粒数、行数、粒大小等多性状参数。该流水线基于视频转点云平台，结合COLMAP、NeRF、密度分离、PCA对齐、柱面展开、Cellpose-SAM无微调实例分割等技术，在168个玉米穗的留出集上取得了粒数R^2=0.921、行数MAE=0.75的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玉米籽粒性状（行数、行粒数、粒大小）受遗传因素影响显著且与产量相关，但人工测量速度无法跟上育种项目中产生的大量玉米样本，亟需高效自动化的表型提取方法。

Method: 流水线流程：1) 通过COLMAP和NeRF处理原始视频帧生成3D点云；2) 基于密度分离隔离果穗；3) 将点云距离校准为物理单位；4) 利用PCA沿Z轴对齐；5) 柱面展开成2D图像；6) 增强对比度并使用Cellpose-SAM进行零微调实例分割；7) 采用三重拼接展开策略防止接缝处籽粒重复计数。

Result: 在268个标注穗中划分的168穗留出集上，籽粒计数R^2=0.921（MAPE=10.33%）；粒行数误差在±2行以内的占比95.2%（MAE=0.75行）。生成的多性状数据集每个果穗均具有已知基因型身份，可直接用于表型-基因型关联分析。

Conclusion: 该全自动流水线能够准确、高效地从3D点云中提取玉米果穗关键籽粒性状，解决了人工测量瓶颈问题，生成的数据集具备基因型信息，为后续开展数量性状关联分析（如GWAS）奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: Maize kernel traits such as row number, kernels per row, and kernel size vary largely for genetic reasons and are consistently associated with regions of the genome that influence yield. Manual measurement of these traits, however, cannot keep pace with the volume of maize generated in a breeding program. To address this, we developed and validated a fully automated pipeline for extracting these traits from 3D point clouds of corn ears, built on a recently developed video-to-point-cloud platform. Raw video frames are processed through COLMAP and NeRF, the ear is isolated via density-based separation, and the point cloud is distance-calibrated to physical units. The calibrated ear point cloud was Z-axis aligned via PCA and cylindrically unwrapped to a 2D image. We enhanced contrast and performed zero-fine-tuning instance segmentation using Cellpose-SAM. A triple-juxtaposed unwrap strategy was used to prevent double-counting at the seam. The pipeline achieved kernel count R^2 = 0.921 (MAPE = 10.33%) and kernel row number within +-2 rows for 95.2% of ears (MAE = 0.75 rows) on a 168-ear held-out set from the 268-ear labeled dataset. The resulting multi-trait dataset has known genotype identity for each ear, positioning it for phenotype-to-genotype association analyses.

</details>


### [28] [Learning with Volterra Neural Networks: A System Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01928)
*Haoyu Yun,Hamid Krim,Yufang Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出kVNN（可学习的核化Volterra神经算子），通过核化技巧对Volterra型高阶滤波进行紧凑建模，将不同交互阶数解耦为独立的可学习中心和系数，避免显式高阶张量参数化，实现CNN兼容层，在视觉任务上取得了良好的精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶交互分量对信号、图像和视频建模至关重要，但显式的高阶算子常常面临参数数量和计算成本随阶数快速增长的难题，需要一种既能保持高阶表达能力又足够高效的建模方法。

Method: 本文将Volterra滤波的阶数结构与可学习的多项式核原子相结合，通过核化技巧实现不同交互阶数的解耦表示（各阶由独立的可学习中心和系数表征），从而避免显式的高阶张量参数化，并将整个算子实现为CNN兼容层。

Result: 在代表性视觉任务上的实验表明，kVNN相比现有方法在精度与效率之间取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: 核化Volterra神经算子能够以紧凑高效的方式实现高阶滤波，为高阶交互建模提供了一种兼具结构化可解释性与实际可用性的解决方案。

Abstract: Higher-order interaction components are important for signal, image, and video modeling, but explicit high-order operators often suffer from rapidly increasing parameter and computational costs. This paper presents kVNN, a learnable kernelized Volterra Neural operator for compact higher-order filtering. The motivation is to use kernelization to improve the efficiency of Volterra-type neural operators while providing a structured interpretation of their higher-order components. The proposed formulation combines the order-wise structure of Volterra filtering with learnable polynomial-kernel atoms, allowing different interaction orders to be represented by separate learnable centers and coefficients. This order-decoupled representation avoids explicit high-order tensor parameterization and can be implemented as a CNN-compatible layer. Experiments on representative vision tasks show that kVNN achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [29] [Aggregating Neighbor Embedding Projection and Rank-Based Manifold Learning for Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01963)
*Vinicius Atsushi Sato Kawai,Gustavo Rosseto Leticio,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合邻域嵌入投影（UMAP）与基于排序的流形学习方法，通过Borda Count排序聚合策略来改进基于内容图像检索（CBIR）的检索有效性。在多种公开数据集及ResNet152、Swin Transformer、DINOv2三种深度特征上验证了方法的有效性，尤其在基线方法精度不高时提升明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBIR虽然随深度学习取得了显著进展，但在高维特征空间中有效排序相似图像仍具挑战性：成对距离难以捕捉上下文关系，且视觉特征与高层语义之间存在语义鸿沟。现有方法分别利用流形学习和基于排序的细化方法各有优势，但如何将它们互补结合仍是未解决的难题。

Method: 提出一个结合两种策略的框架：(1) 投影策略方面，利用UMAP生成替代的低维特征表示；(2) 排序策略方面，采用基于排序的重排序方法获得另一个排名；最终通过Borda Count排序聚合策略将来自UMAP投影和重排序所得到的排序列表进行融合。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上使用ResNet152、Swin Transformer和DINOv2提取的深度特征进行实验，结果表明提出方法在多种场景下提升了检索效果，尤其是在基线表示难以达到高精度时效果显著。聚合策略常能改善前列检索质量，在不同数据集和特征提取器上取得具有竞争力的MAP和Precision值。

Conclusion: 通过排序聚合将基于投影和基于排序的流形学习策略相结合，可以为图像检索任务提供互补的上下文信息，验证了该框架在CBIR中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Content-based image retrieval (CBIR) has advanced significantly with deep learning, yet effectively ranking similar images remains challenging, particularly in high-dimensional feature spaces, where pairwise distances often fail to capture contextual relationships and the semantic gap between visual features and high-level concepts persists. Manifold learning and rank-based refinement methods have emerged as complementary strategies, respectively improving feature representations and exploiting contextual information embedded in ranked lists, such as neighborhood relationships among images. However, combining these projection-based and rank-based strategies to exploit their complementary properties remains a challenging research problem. To address this, we propose a framework that combines neighbor embedding projections with rank-based manifold learning through rank aggregation. Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) generates alternative low-dimensional feature representations, and ranked lists obtained from UMAP projections and rank-based re-ranking methods are combined using the Borda Count aggregation strategy. Experiments were conducted on several public datasets using deep learning features extracted from ResNet152, Swin Transformer, and DINOv2 models. Results show that the proposed approach improves retrieval effectiveness in several scenarios, particularly when the baseline representation struggles to achieve high precision. The aggregation strategy also often improves the quality of top-ranked positions, leading to competitive Mean Average Precision (MAP) and Precision values across different datasets and feature extractors. These findings suggest that combining projection-based and rank-based manifold learning strategies through rank aggregation can provide complementary contextual information for image retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [30] [Morphology signal in whole slide image foundation models can automatically triage slides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01987)
*Ayushi Sinha,Shashank Yadav,Benjamin Holmes,Pravat Das,Aaron W. Bogan,James S. Lewis,Santiago Romero-Brufau,Andrew Y. K. Foong,Scott H. Kaufmann,Kathryn M. Van Abel,David M. Routman,Michael R. Lucas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用公开可用的全切片图像（WSI）基础模型（FMs）进行零样本分类的流水线，用于自动筛选癌症诊断中每个患者生成的多个WSI中的肿瘤切片。该方法通过排名评估基准，在多个数据集上证明肿瘤切片能进入前2名（即使患者有高达43张切片），避免人工病理学家繁重标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症诊断和分期过程中，每位患者通常会产生多个WSI，而训练模型前需人工筛选含有肿瘤或诊断性生物标志物的切片，这一步骤需要经验丰富的病理学家进行繁琐的人工整理。现有数据集常人为假设每位患者仅1张切片，或使用患者全部切片训练模型，但这会稀释少数含肿瘤切片的信号。因此需要自动化切片分诊方法。

Method: 提出一种流水线，利用公开可用的WSI基础模型（FMs）进行零样本分类，对每位患者的WSI进行排名，以自动识别含有肿瘤的切片。同时提出了一个排名评估公式/基准，用于衡量基础模型在切片分诊任务中的性能。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估表明，基于WSI FMs的零样本分类预测对WSI进行排名，能够准确识别含最多肿瘤的切片。肿瘤切片在患者有高达43张切片的情况下仍被识别在前2位排名中。

Conclusion: WSI基础模型包含足够的形态学信号，可以自动分诊/筛选切片，从而消除人工标注的需求，克服了多切片场景下模型训练的信号稀释问题。

Abstract: Patient exams in the cancer diagnosis and staging process typically generate several whole slide images (WSIs). One of the initial steps in training models on WSI data is identifying one or a few slides containing tumor or other diagnostic biomarkers necessary for downstream prediction tasks such as estimating recurrence risk or progression-free survival. This step requires tedious manual curation by experienced pathologists. Many published datasets make the artificial assumption of 1 slide per patient. Alternatively, all slides per patient may be used for model training, which may dilute the signal from the few slides containing tumor or other relevant information. In this paper, we present a pipeline to overcome these challenges using publicly available WSI foundation models (FMs). Our evaluations show that ranking WSIs based on predictions from zero-shot classification using WSI FMs accurately identifies slides with the most tumor, indicating that WSI FMs contain sufficient morphology signal to automatically triage slides. We also present a formulation for ranked evaluation to benchmark FM performance in slide triage. We show, on multiple datasets, that tumor slides are identified in the top-2 ranked slides for patients with up to 43 slides.

</details>


### [31] [Linear Fusion MultiDiffusion for Fast Training-Free Spherical Panorama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01997)
*Akio Hayakawa,Yusuke Mukuta,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LF-MultiDiffusion，一种无训练的全景图生成方法，通过将潜伏聚合重构为正则化最小二乘问题并使用Krylov迭代求解器在去噪循环内高效求解，支持目标与参考图像空间间的线性投影，在提升视觉质量和全景一致性的同时实现15.36倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练免费的全景图生成方法（如MultiDiffusion）在映射空间和生成稳定性上存在局限，需要大量透视视图来维持全景一致性，导致生成器和推理效率低下，缺乏更自然、更密集的映射关系。

Method: 将MultiDiffusion扩展到支持目标图像空间与参考图像空间之间的线性投影；将潜伏聚合公式化为正则化最小二乘问题，并在去噪循环内部使用基于Krylov的迭代求解器高效求解，从而支持比先前方法更密集、更自然的映射，并减少所需透视视图数量。

Result: 实验表明，LF-MultiDiffusion在视觉质量、文本对齐和全景一致性上均优于最强的无训练基线方法，同时提供了15.36倍的推理速度提升。

Conclusion: 通过Krylov迭代求解器对正则化最小二乘潜伏聚合的高效求解，LF-MultiDiffusion在减少生成器评估次数、提升推理效率的同时保持了更优的全景生成质量，为无训练全景生成提供了更具实用性的方案。

Abstract: We propose LF-MultiDiffusion, a training-free panorama generation method that extends MultiDiffusion to support linear projections between target and reference image spaces. Our key idea is to reformulate latent aggregation as a regularized least-squares problem and solve it efficiently with a Krylov-based iterative solver inside the denoising loop. This formulation enables denser and more natural mappings than prior training-free methods, yielding more stable generation with far fewer perspective views. As a result, LF-MultiDiffusion reduces the number of image generator evaluations during denoising and significantly improves inference efficiency. Experiments show that LF-MultiDiffusion achieves better visual quality, text alignment, and panoramic consistency than the strongest training-free baseline, while providing a 15.36$\times$ speedup. Our project page is available at: https://ahykw.github.io/lfmd.

</details>


### [32] [Who Drives the Probability Game of VLMs? A Temporal Causal Drive Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02000)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Haoyu Niu,Ke Chao,Changwei Xu,Xinran Duan,Chaoyong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个因果和时间维度的评估框架，通过结构因果模型中的干预和后门调整，推导出三种逐步索引的因果驱动力指标（VCD/QCD/PCD），用于刻画视觉-语言模型（VLM）自回归解码过程中不同信息源（视觉输入、问题文本、生成前缀）的演变作用，无需参考答案即可实现源级诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估指标主要关注最终答案质量，无法揭示不同信息源（视觉、问题、前缀）在生成过程中各自的贡献与作用演变。作者希望填补这一空白，提供超越端到端准确率的、基于生成过程的分析工具。

Method: 基于结构因果模型（SCM）构建框架，采用因果干预和后门调整方法，推导出三个逐步索引的因果驱动力指标：Visual Causal Drive (VCD)、Question Causal Drive (QCD) 和 Prefix Causal Drive (PCD)。这些指标无需参考答案即可计算，用于在自回归解码的每个时间步刻画各信息源的因果影响。另提出prefix-visual imbalance score用于区分先验驱动与视觉驱动的生成。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上的MAVIS、LLaVA-Video-178K和MiraData数据集，以及InternVL2-8B的跨模型验证中，观察到了一致模式：早期问题与视觉引导较强，随后逐步转向对生成前缀的更大依赖。随机化干预验证中，QCD和PCD分别比观测性PMI基线降低34.8%和47.1%的恢复误差；在VLMBias上，prefix-visual imbalance得分达到0.767 AUROC和0.873 AUPRC。

Conclusion: 因果驱动力轨迹为多模态生成提供了补充性的源级诊断工具，揭示了模型在不同时间步骤对各种信息来源的依赖模式变化，具有不依赖参考答案、可跨模型和数据集泛化的优势。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated on complex image and video understanding tasks, yet conventional metrics primarily assess final-answer quality and reveal little about how different information sources shape the generation process. We propose a causal and temporal evaluation framework that traces the evolving roles of visual input, question text, and generated prefixes during autoregressive decoding. Grounded in a Structural Causal Model, we use interventions and backdoor adjustment to derive three step-indexed causal-drive metrics---Visual Causal Drive (VCD), Question Causal Drive (QCD), and Prefix Causal Drive (PCD)---for characterizing source-specific generation patterns without requiring reference answers. Experiments on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct across MAVIS, LLaVA-Video-178K, and MiraData, together with cross-model validation on InternVL2-8B, reveal a consistent transition from stronger early question and visual guidance toward increasing reliance on generated prefixes. Randomized-intervention validation shows that QCD and PCD reduce recovery error over observational PMI baselines by 34.8\% and 47.1\%, respectively. On VLMBias, the prefix--visual imbalance score achieves 0.767 AUROC and 0.873 AUPRC for distinguishing prior-driven from visually grounded generations. These results show that causal-drive trajectories provide complementary source-level diagnostics for multimodal generation.

</details>


### [33] [InsightSeg: Reusing Correction Insights for Guideline-Consistent Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02002)
*Vanshika Vats,Ashwani Rathee,James Davis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsightSeg提出了一种情景记忆机制，将多智能体语义分割系统中成功的更正经验蒸馏为可复用的视觉锚定洞察，在下一次预测前检索相关洞察以预防而非纠正反复出现的指南违规错误，从而在Waymo和Cityscapes上同时提升首次预测和最终分割质量并减少细化步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体细化系统在语义分割中虽然能够通过批判智能体检测和纠正与文本指南不一致的错误，但它们是无状态的：批判反馈被丢弃，导致相同指南特定的错误在整个数据集中被反复发现和纠正，浪费额外的细化成本。作者旨在打破这种"每次从头纠错"的低效循环。

Method: InsightSeg包含三个核心组件：(1) 情景记忆机制将成功的更正过程转化为可复用的视觉锚定洞察；(2) 元分析器将每个合格更正情景蒸馏为指令性的自然语言洞察，并使用补丁级视觉概念向量将其锚定到引发错误的局部图像区域；(3) 在后续图像上，通过密集补丁嵌入匹配这些视觉概念以检索相关洞察，并在分割智能体进行首次预测前用其进行条件化。

Result: 在Waymo和Cityscapes数据集上的实验表明，InsightSeg同时提升了首次通过（first-pass）和最终指南一致的分割性能，同时需要的细化步骤更少，实现了更高的准确性和效率。

Conclusion: 多智能体细化系统可以通过借鉴过去的修正经验，从"纠正重复错误"转变为"预防错误发生"，从而变得更加准确和高效。

Abstract: Guideline-consistent semantic segmentation requires more than category recognition, as real-world labeling policies demand fine-grained, task-specific decisions. Recent multi-agent refinement systems improve compliance with such textual guidelines by detecting and correcting errors. However, they are stateless: feedback from the critiquing agent is discarded, causing the same guideline-specific mistakes to be repeatedly rediscovered and corrected across the dataset at the cost of additional refinement. We introduce InsightSeg, an episodic memory mechanism that converts successful correction episodes into reusable, visually grounded insights. A meta-analyzer distills each qualifying episode into directive natural-language insights and anchors them to the local image regions that caused the error using patch-level visual concept vectors. On subsequent images, these concepts are matched against dense patch embeddings to retrieve relevant insights, which condition the segmenting agent before making its first prediction. This shifts the system from correcting recurring errors to preventing them, improving segmentation quality before any refinement occurs. Across Waymo and Cityscapes, InsightSeg improves both first-pass and final guideline-consistent segmentation performance while requiring fewer refinement steps, demonstrating that multi-agent refinement can become more accurate and efficient by drawing on past correction experience.

</details>


### [34] [InstEditSeg: Instruction-Driven Image Editing for Polyp and Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02004)
*Ziquan Liu,Zhewei Zhu,Xuyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstEditSeg，一个统一的生成式框架，将医学图像分割（息肉与皮肤病变）重构为指令驱动的图像编辑问题，利用潜在扩散模型生成彩色覆盖叠加图而非独立掩码，并通过DINOv3特征引导块融合判别性先验，实现与强判别基线相当的精度以及更优的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有息肉和皮肤病变分割方法面临低对比度、边界模糊和跨域分布差异等挑战。判别式网络和大多数基于扩散的分割方法仅预测独立的二值掩码，未能充分利用大规模预训练生成模型的视觉先验，且难以弥合自然图像与医学图像之间的域差距。
