Method: InstEditSeg将医学分割重构为指令驱动的图像编辑问题：模型不在原始图像上输出掩码，而是基于文本指令在原始图像上渲染颜色编码覆盖层。具体包括：(1) 利用潜在扩散模型条件化图像编辑，使输出与自然图像分布对齐以缓解域差距；(2) 引入DINOv3作为辅助视觉编码器，并设计DINO特征引导块构建多尺度特征金字塔；(3) 通过通道拼接和零初始化卷积将特征金字塔融合到扩散U-Net中，在不扰动预训练权重的情况下注入层次化判别性先验；(4) 采用双分支无分类器引导策略，每个去噪步骤仅需两次前向传播以降低推理成本。

Result: 在息肉和皮肤病变基准上，该方法实现了与强判别式基线相当的精度，并展现出生成式公式的具体优势：在未见数据上具有显著更优的跨域泛化能力、更完整的多发灶分割、指令条件任务控制以及采样灵活性。论文还分析了该范式的局限性，包括颜色敏感性和不支持属性条件选择。

Conclusion: 生成式扩散框架在医学分割中具备竞争力，尤其在跨域泛化和灵活任务控制方面优于判别式方法，同时需要正视其对颜色属性的敏感性等局限。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of polyps and skin lesions is pivotal for clinical diagnosis, yet existing methods struggle with low contrast, ambiguous boundaries, and cross-domain distribution discrepancies. Discriminative networks and most diffusion-based segmentation approaches predict standalone binary masks, leaving the visual priors of large-scale pretrained generative models largely unexploited. We propose InstEditSeg, a unified generative framework that reformulates medical segmentation as an instruction-driven image editing problem. Instead of emitting a mask, the model renders a color-coded overlay on the original image, conditioned on a textual instruction, so that the edited output aligns with the natural image distribution learned by latent diffusion models and mitigates the domain gap between natural and medical imagery. To recover fine anatomical structures, we introduce DINOv3 as an auxiliary visual encoder and a DINO Feature Guidance Block that builds a multi-scale feature pyramid. The pyramid is fused into the diffusion U-Net by channel concatenation and zero-initialized convolution so that hierarchical discriminative priors can be injected without perturbing the pretrained weights. A dual-branch classifier-free guidance strategy requiring only two forward passes per denoising step reduces inference cost. On polyp and skin lesion benchmarks the framework achieves accuracy competitive with strong discriminative baselines, and it further demonstrates concrete advantages of the generative formulation: notably better cross-domain generalization on unseen data, more complete multi-lesion segmentation, instruction-conditioned task control, and sampling flexibility. We also analyze the strengths and limitations of the paradigm, including its color sensitivity and unsupported attribute-conditioned selection. Code is available at: https://github.com/wincharm001/InstEditSeg.

</details>


### [35] [GeoStore: Finding Small Storefronts in Large Scenes -- A Fine-Grained POI Localization Benchmark with Global-to-Local Asymmetric Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02012)
*Lu Han,Xiting Sun,Hao Wang,Zhiqiang Cao,Ruihuan Du,Ziquan Zeng,Chunlong Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoStore，据作者所知是首个专门面向非对称、细粒度、开集POI本地化（用近景店铺照片匹配大规模街景图像）的基准，并进一步提出GLAM全局到局部非对称匹配方法，在Recall@k和mAP上超越强基线和两阶段方法，同时大幅降低重排特征与配对匹配成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉地点识别（VPR）范式假设对称的、整图级别的同场景匹配，但POI本地化实际上是近景查询（目标充满画面）与广角参考（POI仅占小且偏中心外的区域）之间的非对称、细粒度、开集匹配问题；现有全局描述子方法在该任务上系统性受限，因为单一全局向量会稀释小目标信息。因此需要专门的基准和针对性的方法。

Method: 1) 构建GeoStore基准，定义并量化非对称、细粒度、开集的POI本地化任务。2) 提出GLAM方法：将检索锚定（retrieval-anchoring）的全局描述子与非对称局部通路相结合——每个参考图被表示为一组紧凑的池化区域token，查询侧则用单个探针与其通过可学习的软晚期交互（soft late interaction）进行匹配；推理阶段利用相同的token进行轻量级互最近邻（mutual-nearest-neighbor）重排序。

Result: GLAM在Recall@1/5/10和mAP上超过了强全局描述子与两阶段基线方法；重排特征规模小约5倍，每对匹配成本比先前的局部重排方法低约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 对称VPR范式不适用于POI本地化任务，需要非对称的全局-局部匹配策略；GeoStore为该问题提供了首个可复现的评测基准，GLAM展示了高效且有效的解决方案，且基准与代码将公开释放以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Point-of-interest (POI) localization -- matching a user's close-up storefront photograph against large-scale geo-tagged street-view imagery -- underpins map construction, POI verification, and location-based services. Its closest existing paradigm, visual place recognition (VPR), assumes symmetric, whole-image matching of the same scene at a comparable scale; POI localization instead must match a close-up query, in which the target fills the frame, against wide references in which the same POI occupies only a small, off-center region among visually similar shops, under a substantial capture-domain gap. We introduce GeoStore, to our knowledge the first benchmark dedicated to this asymmetric, fine-grained, open-set formulation, and show that global-descriptor methods tuned for symmetric VPR are systematically limited on it, since a single global vector dilutes the small target. We further propose GLAM (Global-to-Local Asymmetric Matching), which couples a retrieval-anchoring global descriptor with an asymmetric local pathway: each reference is kept as a compact set of pooled region tokens and matched against a single query probe through a learnable soft late interaction; at inference, the same tokens enable a lightweight mutual-nearest-neighbor re-ranking. GLAM surpasses strong global and two-stage baselines on Recall@1/5/10 and mAP, with ~5x smaller re-ranking features and ~two orders of magnitude lower per-pair matching cost than prior local re-ranking. The benchmark and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [36] [Detecting Object Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via Cross-Modal Attention Drifts and Mask-Based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02028)
*Xuanbing Wen,Boxu Chen,Le Yang,Jiakai Wang,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CADMP，一种轻量级物体幻觉检测框架，通过结合相邻层跨模态注意力漂移与目标视觉掩蔽下的预测敏感性来识别LVLM中的幻觉输出，在多个基准和开源模型上取得稳定且有竞争力的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中物体幻觉问题严重阻碍其可靠部署。现有检测方法通常仅利用单层注意力的视觉接地信息，未充分探索其跨层演化过程，缺乏对视觉接地动态变化的刻画。

Method: CADMP在解码过程中：(1) 量化相邻跨模态注意力图之间的分布变化，捕捉视觉接地的突变过渡；(2) 选择漂移最大的转换点，定位对应的视觉相关区域；(3) 对这些区域进行掩蔽后测量预测概率变化。注意力漂移信号刻画内部视觉接地的稳定性，概率变化信号验证预测是否真正依赖被识别的视觉证据，两者互补，由轻量级检测器融合以识别幻觉预测。

Result: 在多个基准和代表性开源LVLM上的实验表明，CADMP实现了一致且有竞争力的检测性能；消融研究进一步验证了相邻层漂移建模与基于掩蔽的接地验证之间的互补贡献。

Conclusion: CADMP通过双信号互补（注意力跨层漂移+掩蔽概率变化）有效提升幻觉检测性能，且框架轻量，不依赖昂贵的外部分类器或生成模型。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs), object hallucination remains a major barrier to their reliable deployment. Existing detection methods often characterize visual grounding using attention from individual layers, leaving its evolution across layers underexplored. We propose CADMP, a lightweight object hallucination detection framework that combines adjacent-layer cross-modal attention drift with prediction sensitivity to targeted visual masking. During decoding, CADMP quantifies distributional changes between consecutive cross-modal attention maps to capture abrupt transitions in visual grounding. It then selects the transition with the largest drift, locates the corresponding visually relevant regions, and measures the change in prediction probability after masking these regions. These two signals provide complementary evidence: attention drift characterizes the stability of internal visual grounding, while probability variation verifies whether a prediction truly depends on the identified visual evidence. A lightweight detector integrates both signals to identify hallucinated predictions. Experiments on multiple benchmarks and representative open-source LVLMs demonstrate that CADMP achieves consistently competitive detection performance. Ablation studies further confirm the complementary contributions of adjacent-layer drift modeling and mask-based grounding verification.

</details>


### [37] [SelfLift: Accelerating Few-Step Diffusion via Self-Recovering Resolution Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02036)
*Tingyan Wen,Chenqian Yan,Xurui Peng,Xiazhang Fang,Shuai Wang,Xueqian Wang,Songwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SelfLift，一种自恢复的渐进分辨率推理框架，用于少步扩散模型。通过Artifact-Aware Consistency Lift（免训练，利用直接潜在变量提升与像素VAE重编码之间的不一致性作为伪影风险信号和模型原生修正方向）实现可靠的后期分辨率过渡，并在此基础上通过On-Policy Self Recovery从内部自教师处迁移密集高分辨率指导。在FLUX.2-Klein和Z-Image-Turbo上分别减少41.5%和44.1%的端到端延迟，结合时间步蒸馏实现29.61x和19.21x的总体加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散模型的时间计算已大幅压缩，单次模型评估的空间成本成为推理延迟的主要来源。渐进分辨率推理虽可在低分辨率下进行早期去噪、将高分辨率计算留作精修，但现有方法直接提升中间潜在变量，依赖后续步骤吸收分布不匹配。在少步场景下恢复预算有限，误差残留为可见伪影，限制了过渡时机和效率。

Method: SelfLift由两部分组成：1) SelfLift-zero提出免训练的Artifact-Aware Consistency Lift，利用直接潜在变量提升与像素VAE重编码之间的不一致性作为局部伪影风险信号和模型原生修正方向，实现无需外部超分、额外去噪器或采样调度修改的可靠后期过渡；2) SelfLift-rich在稳健过渡基础上执行On-Policy Self Recovery，在student访问状态上从内部自教师迁移密集高分辨率指导，与渐进分辨率动力学保持一致。

Result: 在FLUX.2-Klein和Z-Image-Turbo上，SelfLift分别将端到端延迟降低41.5%和44.1%。结合时间步蒸馏，分别实现29.61x和19.21x的总体加速（相对于对应的50步模型），同时保持有竞争力的生成质量，建立了更强的少步扩散速度-质量前沿。

Conclusion: SelfLift通过让生成模型自身提供过渡修复信号和轨迹对齐监督，解决了少步渐进分辨率推理中后期过渡导致的伪影问题，在保持生成质量的同时大幅减少端到端推理延迟，为少步扩散模型建立了更强的速度-质量权衡边界。

Abstract: Few-step diffusion models substantially compress temporal computation, making the spatial cost of each model evaluation an increasingly dominant source of inference latency. Progressive-resolution inference reduces this cost by performing early denoising at low resolution and reserving high-resolution computation for refinement. However, existing methods typically lift intermediate latents directly and rely on subsequent steps to absorb the induced distribution mismatch. In the few-step regime, the limited recovery budget leaves these errors as visible artifacts, constraining how late the transition can occur and, consequently, how efficiently it can be performed. We introduce SelfLift, a self-recovering progressive-resolution framework that derives both transition-repair signals and trajectory-aligned supervision from the generative model itself. SelfLift-zero proposes a training-free Artifact-Aware Consistency Lift, using disagreement between direct latent lifting and pixel-VAE re-encoding as both a localized artifact-risk signal and a model-native correction direction. It enables reliable late transitions without external super-resolution, extra denoiser evaluations, or sampling-schedule modifications. Building on this robust transition, SelfLift-rich performs On-Policy Self Recovery on student-visited states, transferring dense high-resolution guidance from an internal self-teacher while remaining aligned with the altered progressive-resolution dynamics. Across FLUX.2-Klein and Z-Image-Turbo, SelfLift reduces end-to-end latency by 41.5% and 44.1%, respectively. Combined with timestep distillation, it delivers overall speedups of 29.61x and 19.21x over the corresponding 50-step models while preserving competitive generation quality, establishing a stronger speed-quality frontier for few-step diffusion.

</details>


### [38] [Test-Time Logit Prompting for Source-Free Missing Modality Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02039)
*Taixi Chen,Nancy Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TLP（Test-Time Logit Prompting），一种轻量级无源测试时自适应框架，用于视觉语言模型在缺失模态场景下的识别任务。通过不确定性感知调整和模态完整一致性正则化优化logit提示，仅需数百个可调参数和少量测试时优化步骤即可显著提升识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中视觉语言模型常面临模态缺失输入，导致性能显著下降。现有方法依赖源训练数据学习模态补偿策略，但在隐私、存储或可访问性受限（如临床应用、个性化AI服务）时难以应用。因此，如何在无需源训练数据的情况下，高效地在测试时自适应视觉语言模型以应对缺失模态，是一个重要且未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 提出测试时Logit提示（TLP）框架，这是一种轻量级无源测试时自适应方法。TLP通过不确定性感知调整（uncertainty-aware adjustment）和模态完整一致性正则化（modality-complete consistency regularization）来优化logit提示，从而适应性地调整预测置信度，同时保持语义一致性。

Result: 在多种视觉语言基准上的广泛实验表明，TLP在缺失模态场景下持续提升识别性能，最高可获得8%的改进，同时仅需数百个可调参数和少量测试时优化步骤。

Conclusion: TLP有效解决了视觉语言模型在缺失模态和无法访问源训练数据条件下的识别难题，提供了一种高效、轻量且无需源数据的测试时自适应解决方案，在效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable performance by leveraging complementary information from large-scale image-text pairs. However, missing-modality inputs are commonly encountered during real-world deployment, often leading to significant performance degradation. Existing methods primarily enhance model robustness by learning modality compensation strategies from source training data. However, their reliance on source training data makes them difficult to apply when original data are unavailable due to privacy, storage, or accessibility constraints, such as clinical applications and personalized AI services. This raises an important yet underexplored question: can VLMs be efficiently adapted at test time for visual recognition with missing modalities without accessing source training data? To this end, we propose Test-Time Logit Prompting (TLP), a lightweight source-free test-time adaptation framework for visual recognition with missing modalities. To address missing-induced prediction shifts, TLP optimizes logit prompts with uncertainty-aware adjustment and modality-complete consistency regularization, adaptively adjusting prediction confidence while preserving semantic consistency. Extensive experiments across diverse vision-language benchmarks demonstrate that TLP consistently enhances recognition performance under missing-modality scenarios, achieving up to 8\% improvements while requiring only hundreds of tunable parameters and a few test-time optimization steps.

</details>


### [39] [LaST-SR: Laplace-Inspired Steady-Transient Complex-Frequency Decomposition for Single Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02063)
*Linhao Li,Zhaojie Pan,Langkun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LaST-SR，一种基于复频率分解的单图像超分辨率方法。该方法首次为二维特征图推导出近似的稳态-瞬态分解，通过并行设计全局全频谱傅里叶分支和窗口条件化局部复频率分支，分别建模图像的周期性全局依赖与非周期性局部变化，并设计稳态-瞬态协同聚合模块进行特征融合，在五个基准上实现了最优的×2和×4超分辨率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于傅里叶算子的SISR方法依赖周期性谱基，难以表达局部非周期性变化，限制了不规则结构和精细细节的恢复。作者借鉴动力系统中拉普拉斯神经算子将傅里叶模式扩展至复频率的思想，希望同时建模周期性全局信息和瞬时/非周期性局部信息，以提升超分辨率重建的结构一致性。

Method: 1) 首次为二维特征图推导近似的稳态-瞬态分解，为复频率分解提供解析基础；2) 设计复频率分解模块（CFD），包含两个分支：全局全频谱傅里叶分支建模图像级依赖和长程结构一致性，窗口条件化局部复频率分支建模局部、内容相关的非周期性变化；3) 设计稳态-瞬态协同聚合模块（STCA）实现跨分支交互和联合特征聚合。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的×2和×4单图像超分辨率实验中，LaST-SR在PSNR/SSIM指标上均优于所比较的方法；消融实验验证了所提出架构和关键建模机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 将动力系统中的复频率/稳态-瞬态分解思想引入SISR是可行且有效的，通过全局周期性与局部非周期性特征的互补建模，能够显著提升超分辨率重建的结构一致性和细节保真度。

Abstract: Single-image super-resolution (SISR) requires global context modeling for structurally consistent reconstruction. Fourier operators are increasingly adopted for global feature modeling. However, their periodic spectral bases constrain the representation of localized aperiodic variations, limiting the recovery of irregular structures and fine details. In dynamical systems, the Laplace neural operator extends Fourier modes to complex frequencies and decomposes the output signal into complementary steady-state and transient responses to jointly model periodic and aperiodic information. We derive, for the first time, an approximate steady-transient decomposition for two-dimensional feature maps, providing an analytical basis for the proposed complex-frequency decomposition. Accordingly, we propose LaST-SR, centered on a Complex-Frequency Decomposition module that couples a global full-spectrum Fourier branch for image-wide dependencies and long-range structural consistency with a window-conditioned local complex-frequency branch for localized, content-dependent aperiodic variations. To fuse the resulting features, we further design a Steady-Transient Collaborative Aggregation module for cross-branch interaction and joint aggregation. Experiments on five benchmarks show that LaST-SR achieves the best PSNR/SSIM among the compared methods for $\times2$ and $\times4$ SISR. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed architecture and its key modeling mechanisms.

</details>


### [40] [DPA: Decoupling Product-Agnostic Anomaly Representations for Zero-shot Anomaly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02075)
*Hang Yao,Yansheng Fu,Ming Liu,Zifei Yan,Yanli Ji,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于异常迁移的零样本异常生成方法（DPA），通过复用已有源产品的真实异常到未见过的目标产品上，利用扩散模型解耦产品无关的异常表征、自适应掩码引导生成及免训练的像素级标注模块，生成更真实的异常样本以提升下游异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测中的新部署产品通常只有正常图像，难以收集异常样本。现有的零样本异常生成方法依赖纹理图像或文本描述作为异常来源，往往产生不真实的异常。作者观察到相似异常可能在不同产品间重复出现，因此提出复用已有源产品的真实异常进行迁移，从而避免为目标产品收集或生成不切实际的异常。

Method: 首先通过异常类型过滤机制选择适合目标产品的源异常类型；然后提出DPA（Diffusion-based framework that decouples Product-Agnostic anomaly representations），利用不匹配数据对训练学习产品无关的异常嵌入，实现跨产品的可迁移异常概念学习；进一步设计自适应掩码引导生成流程，控制生成异常的位置和几何合理性；最后引入免训练的异常标注模块，产生与生成异常对齐的像素级标注。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA和专门的异常迁移基准上的大量实验表明，所提出的设置和DPA方法能生成更真实的异常，并在零样本和少样本设置下显著提升下游异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 异常迁移为零样本异常生成提供了新范式，DPA通过解耦产品无关异常表征和自适应掩码控制，有效解决了跨产品异常迁移中的真实性和合理性难题，为工业异常检测在无目标产品异常样本场景下的应用提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection benefits from anomaly samples, yet newly deployed products typically provide only normal images, making anomaly samples difficult to collect. Zero-shot anomaly generation offers a promising solution which avoids collection of target-product anomalies. However, existing methods mainly rely on texture images or text descriptions as anomaly sources, which often produce unrealistic anomalies. Observing that similar anomalies can recur across different products, we propose anomaly transfer-based zero-shot generation, which reuses real anomalies from existing source products, making target-product anomalies no longer necessary to generate realistic anomalious samples for unseen target products. Since not every anomaly type suits the target product, an anomaly type filtering mechanism first selects plausible source types. To transfer selected anomaly, we propose DPA, a diffusion-based framework that decouples product-agnostic anomaly representations. Instead of directly extracting anomaly representations, DPA learns product-irrelevant anomaly embeddings through training with the mismatched data pair, enabling transferable anomaly concept learning across products. Furthermore, we design an adaptive mask-guided pipeline that leverages adaptive masks to control the positional and geometric plausibility of generated anomalies during generation. A training-free anomaly labeling module is further introduced to produce pixel-level annotations aligned with generated anomalies. Extensive experiments on MVTec-AD, VisA, and a dedicated anomaly-transfer benchmark demonstrate that the proposed setting and DPA generate more realistic anomalies and significantly improve downstream anomaly detection performance under both zero-shot and few-shot settings. Source code and models will be released.

</details>


### [41] [KSG-Net: Key-Sparse and Global-Context Learning for Maritime 3D Ship Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02077)
*Zhouyuan Huai,Meiqi Wan,Yan Yang,Minshi Chen,Xin Yuan,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KSG-Net，一种用于海洋三维船舶检测的关键稀疏与全局上下文学习网络，通过联合增强局部判别特征和全局结构感知，在统一的完全稀疏检测框架内解决小型船舶弱特征表征和大型船舶全局结构建模不足两大难题。在泰晤士河船舶数据集和模拟数据集上验证了其在多尺度船舶检测上的优越性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海洋LiDAR感知中基于2D特征或密集表示的方法难以兼顾检测精度与计算效率，而面向道路场景的稀疏3D检测器泛化能力不足。海洋场景特有的两大挑战为：小型稀疏船舶的弱特征表征问题，以及大型船舶因局部稀疏卷积感受野受限导致的全局结构建模不足问题。

Method: 提出KSG-Net框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 关键稀疏多尺度聚合（KSMA）模块，通过选择信息量丰富的关键体素并聚合跨尺度邻域特征来增强小型稀疏船舶的表示；(2) 全局上下文聚合（GCA）模块，通过场景级上下文建模结合门控残差交互来捕获远程几何依赖关系，改善大型船舶的结构表示。两者统一集成于完全稀疏检测框架中。

Result: 在泰晤士河船舶数据集和模拟数据集上的大量实验表明，KSG-Net在多尺度船舶检测任务中持续优于现有方法，并在复杂海事环境中表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: KSG-Net通过统一的完全稀疏检测框架同时解决小型稀疏船舶的局部特征增强与大型船舶的全局建模问题，有效提升了海事3D船舶检测的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D ship detection in maritime environments is critical for autonomous navigation, yet remains challenging due to large-scale vessel variations, sparse point clouds of small vessels, and severe sea-clutter interference. Existing methods, primarily based on 2D features or dense representations, struggle to balance detection accuracy and computational efficiency, while sparse 3D detectors designed for road scenes generalize poorly to maritime scenarios. This paper focuses on two key challenges in maritime LiDAR perception: weak feature representation for small and sparse vessels, and insufficient global structural modeling for large vessels due to the limited receptive field of local sparse convolutions. To address these issues, we propose KSG-Net, a Key-Sparse and Global-Context learning network for maritime 3D ship detection. The core idea is to jointly enhance local discriminative features and global structural awareness within a unified fully sparse detection framework. Specifically, a Key Sparse Multi-scale Aggregation (KSMA) module is designed to enhance the representation of small and sparse vessels by selecting informative key voxels and aggregating cross-scale neighborhood features. Furthermore, a Global Context Aggregation (GCA) module is introduced to capture long-range geometric dependencies through scene-level context modeling with gated residual interactions, thereby improving the representation of large vessels. Extensive experiments on the Thames River vessel dataset and simulated datasets demonstrate that KSG-Net consistently outperforms existing methods in multi-scale vessel detection and exhibits strong robustness in complex maritime environments.

</details>


### [42] [Rendering-in-the-Loop: An Execution-Driven Agent for Interactive Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02088)

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have achieved remarkable progress in front-end web development, generating interactive webpages from multimodal references such as screenshots and interaction videos. However, existing work largely emphasizes visual metrics such as aesthetics and layout similarity, while overlooking the more critical validation of interactive functionality. We present RILA, an execution-driven agent that puts browser rendering in the loop, iteratively editing generated code from runtime interaction feedback. RILA introduces an Action Interaction Verification (AIV) module that replays the reference interaction trajectory on the generated webpage to collect grounded execution-aware observations, and an Execution-aware Rendering Score (ERS) that jointly measures interaction correctness and visual fidelity to guide iterative optimization. We further build an execution-verified data synthesis pipeline that produces diverse, high-quality training data, offering gains complementary to inference-time optimization. On IWR-Bench, RILA consistently improves both interaction and visual fidelity across foundation models. Notably, with our training pipeline, RILA lifts the compact Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 40.40% to 57.52%, surpassing far larger one-shot generators, including the 1T-parameter Kimi-K2.6 (55.61%) and the proprietary GPT-5.5 (55.74%).

</details>

*Peifeng Liu,Bin Li,Qingsong Zhang,Yangxin Yu,Leqing Chen,Xiaoye Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向文本中心图像篡改取证的证据引导的"检测器-定位器-推理器"三模块级联系统，在ACM Multimedia 2026 GenText-Forensics挑战赛中以0.638的最终得分排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC的快速发展使以文本为核心的图像操纵愈发普遍，带来了新的取证挑战——不仅需要真实性检测，还需要空间定位和基于证据的解释说明，而现有的方法往往只关注单一检测任务，缺乏系统性的证据链支撑。

Result: 在官方隐藏测试集上取得0.638的最终评分，在挑战赛中排名第二，验证了证据引导系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 证据引导的多模块级联系统（检测、定位、推理）在文本中心图像取证任务上表现出色，说明了结合全局检测、空间定位与多模态大模型推理的pipeline能够有效提升综合取证性能。

Abstract: The rapid progress of AIGC has made text-centric image manipulation increasingly accessible, creating new forensic challenges that require not only authenticity detection but also spatial grounding and evidence-based explanation. This paper presents our solution to the GenText-Forensics Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026. We propose an evidence-guided detector-localizer-reasoner system, where an image-level detector provides a global authenticity prior, a dedicated localizer extracts tampered regions as spatial grounding evidence, and an MLLM-based reasoner generates structured forensic reports grounded in this expert forensic evidence. These modules are connected through a cascaded evidence flow: the detector gates the subsequent localization and prompting process, the localizer converts tamper responses into grounding boxes, and the reasoner is trained to synthesize the detector decision and localized evidence into the final report. As a key part of our method, we introduce iterative difficulty-aware mining to improve localization quality and apply report-mask consistency post-processing to align report grounding with predicted masks. On the official hidden test set, our system achieves a final score of 0.638 and ranks second in the challenge, validating the effectiveness of the proposed evidence-guided system. The code is available at https://github.com/peifengLiu42/ACMMM26-evidence-guided-detector-localizer-reasoner-system.

</details>


### [44] [Disease Burden over Skin Tone: Decomposing the Dermatology-AI Generalization Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02111)
*Nirajan Kunwor,Sanjaya Poudel,Quoc-Huy Trinh,Jahidul Arafat,Sunil Kumar Gaire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统探究皮肤科AI模型泛化失败的主因，发现疾病分布偏移比肤色欠表征贡献更大。癌症训练的模型在陌生临床条件下准确率从0.62骤降至0.21，而肤色差距较小且不一致。表征分析显示这种失败源于表征能力局限而非仅输出标签缺失，且皮肤病学预训练特征保留更强的可迁移结构（kNN纯度提升+0.23 vs +0.06）。从皮肤病学基础模型出发，每类约10个标注样本即可恢复大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤科AI模型主要基于浅肤色、以癌症为中心的图像集合训练，却越来越多地被部署在资源受限环境中，而这些环境中的患者在肤色和疾病分布两个混杂轴上与训练人群不同。已有研究表明泛化性能差，但尚不清楚这种失败主要是由肤色欠表征还是疾病分布偏移导致的，厘清根因对模型改进和资源分配至关重要。

Method: 评估三类模型：癌症训练的基线（在HAM10000和ISIC 2019上微调的ResNet-50）、两个皮肤病学基础模型（DermLIP和MONET）和一个通用视觉模型（DINOv3）作为冻结特征提取器。使用两个数据集：(1) 按肤色分层且疾病匹配的DDI数据集；(2) 疾病偏移但肤色多样的SCIN数据集。进行分层准确率比较、无标签表征分析（kNN纯度），以及验证表征质量能否预测轻量适应下的性能恢复。

Result: 疾病分布偏移比肤色贡献更大。癌症基线在迁移到陌生临床条件时平衡准确率从0.62降至0.21，而疾病内肤色差距较小（0.10-0.18）且不一致。表征分析表明癌症专用特征对陌生条件的聚类能力差（kNN纯度提升仅+0.06），而皮肤病学预训练特征保留更强的可迁移结构（+0.23）。从皮肤病学基础模型出发，每类约10个标注样本可恢复大部分可达到的性能。

Conclusion: 皮肤科AI泛化失败的主要根源是疾病分布偏移而非肤色欠表征，这反映的是基础表征能力的局限。皮肤病学基础模型具有更强的可迁移表征，且在极少标注下即可通过轻量适应恢复性能。作者发布了评估协议和代码以支持可重复的皮肤科AI泛化审计。

Abstract: Dermatology artificial intelligence (AI) models are predominantly trained on light-skinned, cancer-focused image collections, yet they are increasingly proposed for deployment in resource-constrained settings where patients differ from training populations along two confounded axes: skin tone and disease distribution. We investigate whether poor generalization is primarily caused by skin-tone underrepresentation or disease-distribution shift. We evaluate a cancer-trained baseline (ResNet-50 fine-tuned on HAM10000 and ISIC 2019), two dermatology foundation models (DermLIP and MONET), and a general-purpose vision model (DINOv3) as frozen feature extractors. Models are evaluated on a tone-stratified disease-matched dataset (Diverse Dermatology Images, DDI) and a disease-shifted tone-diverse dataset (Skin Condition Image Network, SCIN). Our results show that disease-distribution shift contributes more than skin tone in the evaluated settings. The cancer baseline decreases from 0.62 to 0.21 balanced accuracy when transferred to unfamiliar clinical conditions, while the within-disease skin-tone gap is smaller (0.10-0.18) and inconsistent. Label-free representation analysis shows that this failure reflects a representational limitation rather than only missing output labels: cancer-specialized features poorly cluster unfamiliar conditions (kNN purity lift +0.06 over chance), whereas dermatology-pretrained features retain stronger transferable structure (+0.23). Finally, we show that representation quality predicts recoverable performance under lightweight adaptation. Starting from dermatology foundation models, approximately ten labeled examples per clinical category recover most attainable performance. We release the evaluation protocol and code to support reproducible auditing of dermatology AI generalization.

</details>

*Mingxin Liu,Chengfei Cai,Anwen Lu,Pengbo Xu,Jun Li,Jinze Li,Depin Chen,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Φ-Omni框架，基于部分信息分解（PID）理论，通过协同信息瓶颈（SIB）和ΦID目标函数，将自监督学习从传统的对比对齐范式转向协同交互知识蒸馏，在计算病理学的WSI表征学习中超越监督学习和现有SSL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态SSL方法通过对比对齐将异构模态强行映射到统一潜在空间，导致"模态塌缩"——模态间独特的协同诊断信号（Φ）被丢弃，仅保留了琐碎的冗余信息。作者假设最强的任务无关SSL训练信号应来自于蒸馏协同交互而非对齐共享冗余。

Method: 提出Φ-Omni框架，包含：(1) 基于Partial Information Decomposition（PID）理论的协同信息解缠框架；(2) Synergistic Information Bottleneck（SIB），由ΦID目标函数调控；(3) 显式抑制边际冗余同时最大化不可约协同性，蒸馏高阶跨模态交互；(4) 在乳腺癌(n=1031)和肺癌(n=919)队列上进行预训练。

Result: 在5个独立外部数据集、8个任务上，Φ-Omni在少样本场景下显著超越监督学习和现有SSL基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了协同信息蒸馏（而非冗余对齐）是更优的任务无关SSL训练范式，为全模态（组织学+基因组学+临床报告）病理表征学习提供了新的理论驱动框架。

Abstract: In computational pathology (CPath), developing omni-modal self-supervised learning (SSL) models that integrate histology, genomics, and clinical reports enables transferable representation learning for whole slide images (WSIs). Existing approaches implicitly force heterogeneous modalities into a uniform latent space by contrastive alignment, causing modality collapse where unique, synergistic diagnostic signals (termed as $\mathrmΦ$) are discarded in favor of trivial redundancy. We hypothesize that the strongest task-agnostic SSL training signal stems from distilling the synergistic interactions over merely aligning shared redundancy. To this end, we introduce \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni}, a synergistic information disentanglement framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition (PID) theory for slide representation learning. Unlike standard contrastive approaches, \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni} employs a Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SIB) regulated by the proposed $\mathrmΦ\text{ID}$ objective, which explicitly suppresses marginal redundancy while maximizing irreducible synergy, thereby distilling high-order cross-modal interactions. Following pretraining on breast ($n$=1031) and lung ($n$=919) cohorts, \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni} demonstrates superior few-shot performance across five independent external datasets spanning eight tasks compared to supervised and SSL baselines. Source code is available here.

</details>


### [46] [World-Coherent Decoding: Self-Verifying Test-Time Planning for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02159)
*Chuhan Zhang,Seiji Ito,Kenta Hoshino,Satoshi Ikehata,Ikuro Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出World-Coherent-Decoding (WCD)，一种自验证的测试时规划框架，通过流式视频惊异度和动作路径力评估从冻结的世界行动模型(WAM)中采样的多个未来假设，并在执行后用实际观测审计所选想象，训练轻量级在线预测器优化后续候选选择，将延迟自验证转化为执行前可靠性估计，无需更新主干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界行动模型(WAMs)通过随机生成视觉未来并解码动作来控制机器人，但实证观察表明结果强烈依赖于选择了哪个未来。现有方法对未来的选择缺乏可靠的评估机制，导致控制质量不稳定。

Method: WCD在每个决策步骤从冻结的WAM中采样多个候选，使用内部生成信号排序：基于流的视频惊异度评估视觉合理性，动作路径力评估动作生成稳定性。执行后，实际观测与所选想象中的差异（想象-现实不匹配）被用于训练轻量级在线预测器，以改善未来候选的选择。整个过程不更新主干模型。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准上，WCD在有限随机场景监督下将Hard成功率从55.80%提升至60.90%，在Horizon-3任务上获得+16.43的提升，并在真实Franka视觉偏移测试中展现出定性鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展对于WAMs而言，其关键不在于采样更多未来，而在于选择可靠的未来。WCD通过将延迟的自我验证转化为执行前的可靠性估计，验证了这一简单原则的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) aim to control robots by stochastically generating visual futures and then decoding actions, but empirical observations indicate that the results can strongly depend on which future is selected. We propose World-Coherent-Decoding (WCD), a self-verifying test-time planning framework that treats WAM rollouts as falsifiable future--action hypotheses. At each decision step, WCD samples multiple candidates from a frozen WAM and ranks them using internal generative signals: flow-based video surprisal for visual plausibility and action path effort for action-generation stability. After execution, the realized observation audits the selected imagination, yielding an imagination--reality mismatch that trains a lightweight online predictor for future candidate selection. Thus, WCD converts delayed self-verification into pre-execution reliability estimation without updating the backbone model. On RoboTwin 2.0, WCD improves Hard success under limited randomized-scene supervision from $55.80\%$ to $60.90\%$, with a $+16.43$ gains on Horizon-3 tasks, and shows qualitative robustness on real Franka visual-shift tests. These results highlight a simple principle: test-time scaling for WAMs depends less on sampling more futures than on selecting reliable ones.

</details>


### [47] [Progressive Pseudo-Label Optimization for Point-Supervised Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02171)
*Hailong Ning,Hao Wang,Yimeng Wang,Tao Lei,Renwei Dian,Asoke K. Nandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段框架，将SAM2先验引入点监督变化检测（PS-CD），通过逐步自适应解决点标注空间覆盖不足导致的伪标签不完整和噪声问题。Stage I利用SAM2生成对象感知候选掩码并设计双时相掩码选择策略，配合轻量级CNN细化模块提升边界质量；Stage II构建师生自训练框架实现伪标签细化与模型重优化的闭环。在WHU-CD、LEVIR-CD和SYSU-CD三个基准上超越多数弱监督方法，并与部分全监督方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点监督变化检测仅需稀疏点标注即可大幅降低标注成本，但点标注空间覆盖有限，生成的伪标签往往不完整且包含噪声，严重制约检测性能。因此需要引入更强的先验信息（如SAM2）并设计有效的自适应机制来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) Stage I：利用SAM2从双时相图像的点标注生成对象感知候选掩码，设计双时相掩码选择策略将通用分割响应转化为可靠的变化伪标签；随后使用带不确定性感知损失的轻量级CNN细化模块提升边界质量和局部结构一致性。(2) Stage II：构建教师-学生自训练框架，教师模型通过指数移动平均更新并周期性刷新伪标签，形成伪标签细化与模型重优化交替进行的闭环优化过程。

Result: 在WHU-CD、LEVIR-CD和SYSU-CD三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在大多数基准上优于已有的弱监督方法，并且与部分全监督方法相比仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 引入SAM2先验并采用两阶段渐进式自适应策略，能有效缓解点监督变化检测中伪标签不完整和噪声的问题，显著提升检测性能，验证了将大规模预训练分割模型先验迁移至弱监督下游任务的可行性。

Abstract: Point-supervised change detection (PS-CD) aims to identify pixel-level changes between bi-temporal images using only sparsely annotated points. Although point annotations substantially reduce labeling costs, their limited spatial coverage often results in incomplete and noisy pseudo-labels. To address this issue, we propose a two-stage framework that introduces SAM2 priors into PS-CD and progressively adapts them to the target task. In Stage I, SAM2 generates object-aware candidate masks from point annotations on the bi-temporal images, and a bi-temporal mask selection strategy is designed to convert generic segmentation responses into more reliable change pseudo-labels. Subsequently, a lightweight CNN refinement module with an uncertainty-aware loss is employed to improve boundary quality and local structural consistency. In Stage II, we construct a teacher-student self-training framework in which the teacher is updated by exponential moving average and periodically refreshes the pseudo-labels. This design establishes a closed-loop optimization process that alternates between pseudo-label refinement and model re-optimization. Experiments on three benchmark datasets, including WHU-CD, LEVIR-CD, and SYSU-CD, demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms previous weakly supervised approaches on most benchmarks and remains competitive with several fully supervised methods.

</details>


### [48] [CC-4DGS: Computational Deformation and Point-Cloud Compression for Storage-Efficient Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02184)
*Kyungdae Park,Chae Eun Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CC-4DGS提出了一种存储高效且可扩展的动态4D高斯溅射框架，通过计算变形场（CDF）和压缩典型点云属性（CCA）两个关键组件，将每个场景的总存储量从数十至数百MB降至20-30MB，在保持实时渲染和与Swift4D等先进方法相当的重建精度的同时，显著提升了存储效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态4D高斯溅射方法依赖大规模多分辨率哈希表和高维高斯属性，导致每个场景需要数十到数百MB的存储空间，存储开销过大，限制了其在实际应用中的可扩展性。

Method: 提出了两个核心创新：(1) 计算变形场（CDF），用确定性的密集哈希编码和紧凑的神经解码器替代大规模可学习多分辨率哈希表，实现变形特征的即时合成，将变形存储降至每场景仅1-3MB；(2) 压缩典型点云属性（CCA）流程，通过条件自编码、选择性量化和残差码本压缩高维球谐函数外观项及辅助高斯属性，实现3-5倍的点云缩减且质量损失可忽略。

Result: 在N3DV和Technicolor Light Field数据集上的广泛实验表明，CC-4DGS实现了与Swift4D等最先进方法相当的重建精度，总存储量降至20-30MB，同时保持实时渲染性能并展现出优异的运行时-内存权衡。

Conclusion: CC-4DGS通过重新思考变形建模和典型属性存储，实现了动态4D高斯溅射的高效压缩表示，在存储效率、渲染质量和实时性能之间取得了良好的平衡，为动态场景表示提供了更具实用价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic four-dimensional (4D) Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a powerful explicit representation for high-quality view synthesis, yet existing methods still require tens to hundreds of megabytes per scene due to their heavy reliance on large multi-resolution hash tables and high-dimensional Gaussian attributes. This paper presents CC-4DGS, a storage-efficient and scalable framework that rethinks both deformation modeling and canonical attribute storage. First, we introduce a computational deformation field (CDF) that replaces large multi-resolution learnable hash tables with deterministic dense hash encoding and compact neural decoders, enabling on-the-fly synthesis of deformation features while reducing deformation storage to only 1--3 MB per scene. Second, we propose a compression of canonical point-cloud attributes (CCA) pipeline that compresses high-dimensional spherical harmonic appearance terms and auxiliary Gaussian attributes via conditional autoencoding, selective quantization, and residual codebooks, achieving 3--5$\times$ point-cloud reduction with negligible quality loss. Together, these components yield a unified representation that preserves real-time rendering performance while reducing total storage to 20--30 MB. Extensive experiments across the N3DV and Technicolor Light Field datasets demonstrate that CC-4DGS achieves reconstruction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods such as Swift4D, while offering significantly improved storage efficiency and favorable runtime-memory trade-offs.

</details>


### [49] [Lightweight Adaptation of General-Purpose VLMs for Multispectral and SAR Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02187)
*Shanji Liu,Kelu Yao,Junxiao Xue,Chenghui Lv,Xiangyang Miao,Yekai Huang,Yaying Chen,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级方法，通过将多光谱和SAR观测渲染为多个光学/SAR视图并借助通用VLM的多图像接口和LoRA适配，实现遥感任务的VLM复用，无需训练新的基础模型。在BigEarthNet-v2基准上达到0.8275 micro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视觉识别、指令跟随和生成方面表现强大，但大多数预训练视觉编码器仅针对三通道自然图像设计，无法直接处理多光谱或合成孔径雷达（SAR）等原生观测数据。传统方案需要专用编码器和领域预训练，阻碍了更强通用检查点的复用。

Method: 1) 将每个观测渲染为五个光学视图和一个SAR视图；2) 在提示中命名各视图；3) 使用LoRA适配语言网络和选定的视觉Transformer块；4) 利用结构化监督将预测类别与传感器证据耦合；5) 构建偏好对（省略真实标签但保留支撑证据），鼓励与观测一致的完整预测。

Result: 在基于BigEarthNet-v2的平衡六类土地覆盖基准上，适配的Qwen3-VL达到0.8275 micro F1。相同协议在所有测试的四种VLM架构上均有效，并泛化到Sen1Floods11洪水验证和BigEarthNet.txt图像描述任务。图像移除和失配控制实验表明模型确实使用了提供的传感器观测数据。

Conclusion: 实验证明通用VLM可以通过渲染输入和紧凑的LoRA适配被重新用于多光谱和SAR任务，无需训练新的基础模型，为跨传感器VLM复用提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) now support strong visual recognition, instruction following, and generation. However, most pretrained visual encoders are built around three-channel natural images and do not directly accommodate observations such as native multispectral measurements or synthetic aperture radar (SAR). Adapting VLMs to these sensors typically requires dedicated encoders and domain pretraining, slowing the reuse of stronger general-purpose checkpoints. We show that the multi-image interface of general-purpose VLMs offers a lightweight alternative. Our protocol renders each observation as five optical views and one SAR view, names them in the prompt, and adapts the language network and selected visual transformer blocks with LoRA. This exposes band composites, spectral indices, and radar backscatter through an existing visual interface. For land-cover recognition, structured supervision couples predicted classes with sensor evidence. We further construct preference pairs in which a true label is omitted while its supporting evidence is retained, encouraging complete predictions that remain consistent with the observations. On a balanced six-class land-cover benchmark derived from BigEarthNet-v2, the adapted Qwen3-VL reaches 0.8275 micro F1. The same input and adaptation protocol improves all four tested VLM architectures and transfers to Sen1Floods11 flood verification and BigEarthNet.txt captioning. Image removal and mismatch controls show that the adapted models use the supplied sensor observations. Together, these results demonstrate that VLMs can be repurposed for multispectral and SAR tasks through rendered inputs and compact LoRA adaptation, without training a new foundation model.

</details>


### [50] [TAME: Temporal-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Text-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02204)
*Uicheol Jung,Juyoung Hong,Hojung Kwon,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAME，一种基于CLIP的文本-视频检索框架，通过稀疏混合专家网络、帧-时间令牌和跨时间交互聚合模块，共同建模帧级视觉结构和时间关系，在多个标准TVR基准上显著优于CLIP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将CLIP等图像-文本模型扩展到视频检索的方法缺乏时间建模能力。视频帧在外观和运动上具有异构性，将所有帧压缩为单一表示会掩盖时间结构和语义转换，导致检索性能受限。

Method: 提出三种核心组件：1）在CLIP双编码器中集成稀疏Mixture-of-Experts层，并在视觉分支采用帧一致路由，使专家按帧级视觉模式特化同时保持视觉-语言对齐；2）引入帧-时间（FT）令牌，聚合全局跨帧信息并反馈给每帧，捕获长程时间依赖而不损害局部细节；3）设计跨时间交互聚合（CTIA）模块，通过分阶段时间过滤与融合来细化帧级句子-视频相似度。

Result: 在MSR-VTT上R@1比CLIP4Clip提升4.0，并在DiDeMo、MSVD、LSMDC和ActivityNet等基准上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: TAME通过联合建模帧级结构和时间关系，有效克服了图像-文本模型扩展至视频时的时序建模瓶颈，验证了稀疏MoE和时间令牌在视频文本检索中的有效性。

Abstract: Text-Video Retrieval (TVR) retrieves videos that match a natural-language query, but extending image-text models such as CLIP to videos is fundamentally limited by the lack of temporal modeling. Videos exhibit frame-wise heterogeneity in appearance and motion, and compressing all frames into a single representation often obscures temporal structure and semantic transitions. To address this, we propose Temporal-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Text-Video Retrieval (TAME), a CLIP-based framework that jointly models frame-level structure and temporal relations. First, we integrate sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers into both CLIP encoders and apply frame-consistent routing on the vision branch so that experts specialize according to frame-level visual patterns while preserving the original vision-language alignment. Second, we introduce Frame-Temporal (FT) tokens that aggregate global cross-frame information and feed it back to each frame, enabling the visual encoder to capture long-range temporal dependencies without harming local details. Third, we design a Cross-Temporal Interaction and Aggregation (CTIA) module that refines frame-wise sentence-video similarities through staged temporal filtering and fusion. Experiments on standard TVR benchmarks show that TAME consistently improves over CLIP-based baselines. On MSR-VTT, it improves R@1 by 4.0 over CLIP4Clip, and also achieves consistent gains on DiDeMo, MSVD, LSMDC, and ActivityNet. The code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/TAME.

</details>


### [51] [LeakageBench: Document-Level Leakage Risk for Redacting Personally Identifiable Information in Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02207)
*Vishnu Prasad Vijaya Kumar,Santhosh Venkatesh,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeakageBench，一个包含500张文档图像和11,954个符合GDPR的PII标注的挑战集，从实体级F1、组别泄漏率和文档级泄漏率三个维度评估OCR管线、OCR依赖检测器和OCR-free视觉语言模型在文档级PII脱敏中的表现。实验显示即使借助Code Interpreter工具将定位F1提升至0.249，页面级泄漏率仍高达0.968，揭示现有方法在确保文档安全发布方面存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的PII脱敏在文档图像（扫描件、截图、PDF渲染）上进行时，OCR错误、布局结构和视觉噪声决定了敏感信息是否被真正移除。而现有PII基准大多以文本为中心，未衡量文档级脱敏风险——只要遗漏一个标识符页面就不安全。因此需要构建能够评测文档级脱敏风险的基准。

Method: 构建LeakageBench挑战集：包含500张文档图像、11,954个GDPR对齐的PII标注，涵盖直接标识符、关联键和上下文重新识别面。随后评估了通用OCR管线、商业及任务适配的OCR依赖检测器、OCR-free视觉语言模型，并采用实体级F1、组别泄漏率和文档级泄漏率等多维度指标进行评测。

Result: Code Interpreter将GPT-5.5的定位F1从0.090提升到0.249，但关键页面级泄漏率仍高达0.968。这表明更强的检测能力和工具辅助虽能改善定位精度，却无法使绝大多数页面达到安全发布标准。

Conclusion: LeakageBench作为文档图像上高召回、空间锚定的PII脱敏诊断基准，揭示了当前检测技术在文档级安全脱敏上的显著差距：更强的检测与工具辅助只能改善局部定位，不足以解决整体泄漏问题，未来需在高召回率与空间定位方面取得突破。

Abstract: Real-world personally identifiable information (PII) redaction often operates on document images---scans, screenshots, and PDF renderings---where OCR errors, layout structure, and visual noise determine whether sensitive information is actually removed. Existing PII benchmarks are mostly text-centric and do not measure document-level redaction risk: a page remains unsafe if even one identifier is missed. We introduce LeakageBench, a challenge set of 500 document images with 11,954 GDPR-aligned PII annotations spanning direct identifiers, linkage keys, and contextual re-identification surfaces. We evaluate generic OCR pipelines, commercial and task-adapted OCR-dependent detectors, and OCR-free vision-language models using entity-level F1, group-wise leakage, and document-level leakage metrics. Code Interpreter raises GPT-5.5 localization F1 from 0.090 to 0.249, but critical page-level leakage remains 0.968. These results show that stronger detection and tool assistance improve localization without making most pages safe for release. LeakageBench provides a diagnostic benchmark for high-recall, spatially grounded PII redaction in document images.

</details>


### [52] [Asymmetric Paired-Annotation Learning for Multi-Structure ULF Pediatric Brain MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02210)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Dang P. M. Cao,Minh Hoang Pham,Khanh Nguyen Vo Ngoc,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍AURA方法，一种基于nnU-Net的不对称监督策略，用于便携式超低场（0.064 T）MRI的儿科神经影像分割。AURA将高场衍生（HF）掩膜和低场编辑（LF）掩膜视为两种不同观察而非可互换的金标准，锚定HF掩膜并利用LF掩膜通过有界可靠性门控进行监督。在LISA 2026挑战赛16例开发集上，集成方法达到Dice 0.7988、HD95 1.8892和ASSD 0.7855。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低场MRI（0.064 T）能扩大儿科神经影像的可及性，但分割面临挑战：解剖边界弱、小结构部分可见，且高场参考可能局部配准错误。现有方法简单地将两类标注视为可互换的金标准，未能处理它们代表不同解剖证据来源的本质差异。

Method: AURA是基于nnU-Net的非对称监督策略，核心思想是将HF掩膜和LF掩膜视为不同的观察而非等价金标准。训练锚定于HF掩膜（作为评分目标），并通过有界可靠性门控整合LF掩膜信息。该门控基于标签不一致性、边界情况、预测不确定性、类别可靠性和训练阶段等多因素动态调节LF掩膜的监督权重。同时采用基线与AURA的集成策略。

Result: 在LISA 2026挑战赛16例开发集上，HF监督基线、AURA及其集成的Dice分数分别为0.7984、0.7950和0.7988；集成方法达到HD95为1.8892、ASSD为0.7855。AURA单独表现略低于基线但集成后最优，表明两者存在互补性。

Conclusion: AURA作为不对称监督策略在ULF MRI分割中提供了初步可行方案，综合集成方法在当前开发集上达到最佳性能，但仍需在隐藏测试集和外部ULF队列上进一步验证其泛化能力。

Abstract: Portable ultra-low-field (ULF) MRI can expand access to pediatric neuroimaging, but segmentation at 0.064 T remains challenging because anatomical boundaries are weakly delineated, small structures may be only partially visible, and high-field references can be locally misregistered. The LISA 2026 Challenge provides two non-equivalent annotations reflecting different sources of anatomical evidence: a highfield-derived (HF) mask defining the scored target and a low-field-edited (LF) mask aligned with visible ULF anatomy. In this challenge report, we describe AURA, an nnU-Net-based asymmetric supervision strategy that treats these annotations as distinct observations rather than interchangeable ground truths. AURA anchors training to the HF mask and incorporates the LF mask through a bounded reliability gate based on label disagreement, boundaries, predictive uncertainty, class reliability, and training stage. On a 16-case development split, the HF-supervised baseline, AURA, and their ensemble achieved Dice scores of 0.7984, 0.7950, and 0.7988, respectively, while the ensemble achieved an HD95 of 1.8892 and an ASSD of 0.7855. These results provide a preliminary evaluation of AURA within the LISA 2026 Challenge and motivate further assessment on the hidden test set and external ULF cohorts. Our code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/minhdang050806/ A-nnU-Net-based-asymmetric-supervision-strategy.

</details>


### [53] [FuDU: A Fuzzy Dual-dimensional Uncertainty Framework for Streaming Active Learning in Industrial Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02212)
*Zhaoyang Wang,Haiyong Chen,Binyi Su,Xinwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模糊双维度不确定性（FuDU）框架的流式主动学习方法，用于在实时工业缺陷检测中挖掘不确定样本以提升检测系统可靠性。该方法通过骨干网络上的原型全局不确定性量化模块评估图像级不确定性，并在检测头集成双熵缺陷不确定性评估器量化盒级不确定性，最终利用模糊推理融合双维度不确定性，实现专家知识驱动的自适应采样决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时工业缺陷检测中，深度学习模型的可靠性至关重要。面对连续工业媒体流中大量不确定样本，现有方法难以有效挖掘并提高检测系统的鲁棒性，因此需要一种能够自适应选择高质量标注样本的主动学习方法。

Method: 本文提出FuDU框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于原型的全局不确定性量化（PGUQ）模块，部署在骨干网络上，通过正常/缺陷特征原型评估图像级不确定性；(2) 双熵缺陷不确定性评估器（DeUE），集成于检测头中用于量化盒级不确定性；(3) 模糊双维度不确定性感知策略，将不确定性建模为系统误差，利用模糊推理融合图像级与盒级双维度不确定性，支持专家知识驱动的自适应采样决策。

Result: 综合实验表明FuDU框架是高效且灵活的，特别适用于核燃料棒缺陷检测等具有挑战性的工业检测任务。代码已公开发布。

Conclusion: FuDU通过融合图像级和盒级双维度不确定性，结合模糊推理实现专家知识驱动的自适应采样，有效提升了流式工业缺陷检测中模型的可靠性，验证了其在应对核燃料棒等苛刻工业检测场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of deep learning models in real-time industrial defect detection is critical for high-stakes quality inspection. To mine uncertain samples within continuous industrial media streams, thereby enhancing the reliability of the detection system, this paper proposes a streaming active learning method based on the Fuzzy Dual-dimensional Uncertainty (FuDU) framework. Specifically, we first design a Prototype-based Global Uncertainty Quantification (PGUQ) module on the backbone to evaluate image-level uncertainty via normal/defective feature prototypes. A Dual-entropy defect Uncertainty Evaluator (DeUE) is then integrated into the detection head to quantify box-level uncertainty. Finally, by modeling uncertainty as systematic error, we propose a fuzzy dual-dimensional uncertainty-aware strategy that leverages fuzzy inference to fuse dual-dimensional uncertainties, enabling expert knowledge-driven adaptive sampling decisions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that FuDU is efficient and flexible, making it well-suited for challenging industrial inspection tasks such as the detection of nuclear fuel rod defects. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/wangzhaoyang-508/FuDU.

</details>


### [54] [Signal or Noise? Auditing Rotation-Induced Saliency Drift in Medical and Aerial Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02224)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EquiGrad-CAM，一种无需训练的包装方法，通过平均旋转视图的归一化显著图来提升Grad-CAM等事后显著性图的旋转等变性。作者发现热图的旋转漂移并非来自通道权重（它们在ResNet-50上完全稳定），而是来自空间激活张量——分类器池化本身丢弃了这些运动。在ImageNet-1K上，EquiGrad-CAM将等变性提升36%–247%，并优于旋转增强训练，同时副产品PEUM可用于衡量解释的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无规范朝向的领域（如组织病理学、航拍图像）中，旋转输入后即使预测不变，Grad-CAM等事后显著性热图仍会发生漂移，这削弱了显著性图作为审计证据的可靠性。作者旨在弄清该漂移是忠实信号还是CAM算子引入的噪声，并提出解决手段。

Method: 首先在算子的每一阶段（而非网络输出）测量等变性，定位漂移来源：发现通道权重阶段最稳定（ResNet-50上完全稳定，因GAP+线性头使类梯度场空间恒定），真正移动的是空间激活张量，且分类器自身池化丢弃了该移动。随后通过因果测试（遮挡漂移像素代价低于遮挡随机像素）确认漂移自由度是被分类器丢弃的信息。基于此提出EquiGrad-CAM：取T个旋转视图，将每个视图的显著图逆旋转到公共规范帧后取平均。该方法无需训练。副产品PEUM按可复现性对解释排序。

Result: 在完整ImageNet-1K验证集上，相比单视图Grad-CAM，等变性提升：ResNet-50 +36.0%，VGG-16 +87.5%，ViT-B/16 +247%。尺度匹配消融证明驱动因素是聚合前的对齐而非聚合位置。它超越旋转增强训练而无需重训练，零样本CLIP提升+145%，在PatchCamelyon和RESISC45上产生旋转一致的解释。PEUM在已拍视图基础上零额外成本提升可复现性排序。

Conclusion: 显著图的旋转漂移由分类器丢弃的自由度携带，因此通过旋转视图对齐再聚合（EquiGrad-CAM）可忠实去除漂移而不是破坏信号。该方法训练无关、即插即用，显著提升等变性并在多个架构/数据集上有效，同时提供PEUM作为可复现性度量。

Abstract: Post-hoc saliency maps such as Grad-CAM are increasingly used to audit why a deployed vision model made a decision, yet the heatmap drifts when the input is rotated, even when the prediction is unchanged. In domains with no canonical orientation, such as histopathology and aerial imagery, this undermines using saliency as evidence. We ask whether that drift is faithful signal or noise introduced by the CAM operator, and answer it by measuring equivariance at every stage of the operator rather than inferring it from the network's output. The instability is not where one would guess: the channel weights are the most rotation-stable stage, and on ResNet-50 exactly stable, because a GAP+linear head makes the class gradient field spatially constant. What moves is the spatial activation tensor, and the classifier's own pooling discards that movement. A causal test confirms the consequence: occluding the pixels whose saliency drifts costs the model less than occluding random pixels, at either orientation. The drift is carried by degrees of freedom the classifier throws away, which is what makes removing it faithful rather than destructive. EquiGrad-CAM is a training-free wrapper that takes T rotated views, inverse-rotates each view's saliency into a common canonical frame, and averages. On the full ImageNet-1K validation set it raises equivariance over single-view Grad-CAM by +36.0% (ResNet-50), +87.5% (VGG-16) and +247% (ViT-B/16); a scale-matched ablation isolates alignment before averaging, not the locus of aggregation, as the driver. It beats rotation-augmented training without retraining, lifts zero-shot CLIP by +145%, and yields rotation-consistent explanations on PatchCamelyon and RESISC45. Its by-product PEUM ranks explanations by how reproducible they are, at no cost beyond the views already taken. Code: https://github.com/Khawaja-Murad/EquiGrad-CAM

</details>


### [55] [InfraPatch: Cross-Task Targeted Grayscale Patch Attacks on Infrared-Adapted Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02233)
*Chengyin Hu,Dingyi Lu,Jiaju Han,Xiang Chen,Weiwen Shi,Jiahuan Long,Yiwei Wei,Jiujiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InfraPatch，一种针对红外视觉-语言模型（IR-VLMs）的白盒、逐实例目标对抗贴片攻击框架。该框架在约5%局部面积预算内优化紧凑单通道贴片，结合代理引导定位与任务自适应语义目标，在图像分类、图像字幕和二元视觉问答任务上实现86%-100%的目标攻击成功率，揭示小型灰度贴片可向不同IR-VLM家族注入选定目标语义的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管IR-VLMs在多模态感知领域前景广阔，但其对定向对抗攻击的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。现有贴片攻击方法主要针对RGB模型或单一下游任务，未能刻画局部扰动是否能诱导IR-VLMs产生预期的语义目标。

Method: InfraPatch框架包含：(1) 白盒、逐实例优化的单通道灰度贴片，面积预算约为局部面积的5%；(2) 代理引导（proxy-guided）的贴片放置策略；(3) 任务自适应的语义目标函数，覆盖图像分类、字幕生成和二元VQA三种任务；(4) 使用DiffV2IR将固定30类COCO子集转化为300张合成红外风格图像进行评估。

Result: 在十个红外适应模型变体上，InfraPatch实现了86%-100%的定向攻击成功率。对CLIP和BLIP-2，代理位置搜索相比优化随机放置分别提升6.67和10.33个百分点；LLaVA-1.5在两种设置下均接近100%饱和。贴片面积和目标消融实验进一步揭示了不同架构与任务格式间的显著脆弱性差异。

Conclusion: 小型灰度贴片能在受控数字威胁模型下向不同IR-VLM家族注入选择的目标语义，凸显了红外多模态系统需要更强的鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: Infrared vision-language models (IR-VLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for multimodal perception under low-visibility conditions, yet their robustness to targeted adversarial attacks remains poorly understood. Existing adversarial patch methods mainly study RGB-based models or a single downstream task and do not characterize whether localized perturbations can induce an intended semantic target in IR-VLMs. We propose InfraPatch, a white-box, per-instance framework for targeted digital grayscale patch attacks against IR-VLMs. InfraPatch optimizes a compact single-channel patch within an approximately 5% local-area budget, combines proxy-guided placement with task-adaptive semantic objectives, and induces target behaviors in image classification, image captioning, and binary visual question answering. We evaluate ten infrared-adapted model variants on 300 synthetic infrared-style images generated by applying DiffV2IR to a fixed 30-category COCO subset, using clean-conditioned targeted success criteria. InfraPatch achieves targeted attack success rates from 86.00% to 100% across the ten variants. On CLIP and BLIP-2, proxy location search improves success by 6.67 and 10.33 percentage points over optimized random placement, respectively; LLaVA-1.5 remains saturated near 100% under both settings. Patch-area and objective ablations further expose substantial differences in vulnerability across architectures and task formats. These results show that small grayscale patches can inject chosen target semantics across IR-VLM families under a controlled digital threat model, motivating stronger robustness evaluation for infrared multimodal systems.

</details>


### [56] [SAUF-Net: Structure--Appearance Representation Learning with Uncertainty Feedback for Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02247)
*Qin Lu,Zheyang Jing,Yujie Yang,Jianwang Li,Chen Yi,Shaofeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAUF-Net是一个用于半监督医学图像分割的框架，通过结构-外观解耦表示学习和不确定性反馈机制，在低标注率场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督医学图像分割方法主要依赖预测层面的一致性来利用未标注数据，忽视了内部特征表示的可靠性。医学图像中目标相关的结构线索容易与不稳定的外观变化纠缠，导致不可靠的伪标签和训练过程中的误差累积，增加了标注成本。

Method: SAUF-Net包含四个核心组件：1) 结构-外观解耦模块(SADM)将瓶颈特征分离为结构和外观表示；2) 解耦引导模块(DGM)将这些表示注入解码过程以增强结构感知分割；3) 外观交换一致性分支鼓励结构表示在外观变化下保持稳定；4) 可靠性图引导的双头判别器(包含有效性头和不确定性头)提供特征级不确定性反馈。

Result: 在ISIC-2016和Kvasir-SEG数据集上的大量实验表明，SAUF-Net在低标注率设置下优于最先进的半监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构-外观解耦和不确定性反馈，SAUF-Net有效解决了半监督医学图像分割中特征表示不可靠导致的误差累积问题，尤其在标注样本极少时表现出色，具有降低医学图像标注成本的潜力。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning has shown great potential for reducing annotation costs in medical image segmentation. However, most existing methods mainly exploit unlabeled data through prediction-level consistency, while the reliability of internal feature representations is often overlooked. In medical images, target-related structural cues are easily entangled with unstable appearance variations, which may lead to unreliable pseudo labels and error accumulation during training. To address these issues, we propose SAUF-Net, a Structure--Appearance Representation Learning with Uncertainty Feedback Network for semi-supervised medical image segmentation. SAUF-Net uses the Structure--Appearance Decomposition Module (SADM) to separate bottleneck features into structural and appearance representations. The Disentangled Guidance Module (DGM) injects these representations into the decoding process to enhance structure-aware segmentation. Meanwhile, the Auxiliary Decoder produces branch-specific predictions for reliability estimation and a fused prediction for appearance-swapped consistency. Furthermore, we introduce an Appearance-Swapped Consistency branch to encourage structural representations to remain stable under appearance variations. We also introduce a reliability-map-guided dual-head discriminator with a Validity Head and an Uncertainty Head to provide feature-level uncertainty feedback. Extensive experiments on ISIC-2016 and Kvasir-SEG demonstrate that SAUF-Net outperforms state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, especially under low-label settings.

</details>


### [57] [Handwriting Trajectory Recovery via Autoregressive Ordered Stroke Instance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02251)
*En-Guang Wang,Yan-Ming Zhang,Fei Yin,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架用于手写轨迹恢复：先通过自回归有序笔画预测恢复有序笔画实例，再重建笔画内部的连续运动。实验表明该有序预测方法优于事后笔画排序，且在全点模型下无需轨迹简化即可超越所有基线，并展示了跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线手写仅保留最终空间墨迹，丢失了笔画顺序、书写方向和笔尖运动等时序信息，导致轨迹恢复本质上存在歧义。现有学习方法直接预测完整字符轨迹，未显式利用手写的笔画级组织信息。作者认为恢复书写过程应遵循书写过程本身，即先恢复有序笔画再重建笔画内运动。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段通过自回归有序笔画预测整合笔画提取和笔画顺序恢复，得到有序笔画实例；第二阶段利用方向相关的结构线索生成笔画内部连续运动，重建完整的书写轨迹。

Result: 在中文字写数据集上，有序预测比事后笔画排序更有效；全点模型无需轨迹简化即可在数值上超越所有对比基线；控制分析显示轨迹采样密度显著影响测量到的恢复性能；额外实验验证了对未见中文字符类别的泛化以及向英文和泰米尔文手写的跨语言扩展能力。

Conclusion: 遵循书写过程本身的两阶段恢复策略，即先恢复有序笔画再重建笔画内运动，比直接预测完整轨迹更有效，且具备良好的泛化性和跨语言扩展性。同时指出轨迹采样密度对性能评估有显著影响，需在研究设计中加以考虑。

Abstract: Handwriting trajectory recovery aims to infer the dynamic writing process hidden behind a static handwritten image. Since offline handwriting preserves only the final spatial ink pattern, temporal information such as stroke order, writing direction, and pen-tip motion is lost, making recovery inherently ambiguous. Existing learning-based methods often directly predict the complete character trajectory without explicitly exploiting the stroke-level organization of handwriting. We argue that recovering the writing process should follow the writing process itself. Accordingly, we propose a two-stage framework that first recovers ordered stroke instances and then reconstructs continuous within-stroke motion. The first stage integrates stroke extraction and stroke-order recovery through autoregressive ordered stroke prediction, while direction-related structural cues further support within-stroke trajectory generation. Experiments on Chinese handwriting show that the proposed ordered prediction is more effective than post-hoc stroke ordering. Even without trajectory simplification, our full-point model achieves numerically better results than those reported by all compared baselines, while a controlled analysis shows that trajectory sampling density substantially affects measured recovery performance. Additional experiments demonstrate generalization to unseen Chinese character categories and cross-language extensibility to English and Tamil handwriting.

</details>


### [58] [T2LSC-Bench: Benchmarking Localized Semantic Control in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02255)
*Yan Wang,Xinyi Hou,Weiguo Lin,Junjun Si,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出T2LSC-Bench基准，用于系统评估文生图模型在指定文本区域内渲染文字时，目标文本语义是否发生"泄漏"到非文本视觉内容中的问题。研究发现现有模型即使能正确渲染文本，也可能在锚定区域之外表达目标文本的语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型的视觉文本基准主要评估可读性、拼写准确性和布局，忽略了一种关键缺陷：目标文本的语义通过非文本视觉内容(如物体、场景元素)泄漏到指定文本区域之外。在产品标签、标识、界面设计等应用中，这种语义泄漏会破坏主体身份和场景语义，构成一个重要但未被充分研究的评估维度。

Method: 构建T2LSC-Bench诊断基准，包含50个种子主体和每模型1,200个提示案例(共7,160张评估图像，覆盖6个模型)。采用因子化设计，变化语义关系、场景开放性、提示模式和语言四个维度。提出双分支评估协议：OCR-VLM进行文本验证，结合结构化VLM语义判断，计算四个指标：Text-at-Anchor Accuracy (TAA)、Semantic Subject Preservation (SSP)、Semantic Leakage Rate (SLR)和Conditional Semantic Leakage Rate (cSLR)。

Result: 在压力测试条件下，SLR从1.2%上升到18.1%，cSLR从1.3%上升到18.2%，而TAA仅从91.4%下降到90.9%，表明准确文本渲染并不保证语义的局部约束。抗泄漏提示可将SLR从16.6%降至8.4%而不降低渲染准确度。在420张图像上的人类验证显示自动标注与人工标注高度一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明，文本渲染准确性与目标文本语义的局部包含之间存在显著差距。即使模型能够正确渲染目标字符串，其语义仍可能通过非文字视觉内容泄漏到指定区域之外。T2LSC-Bench为这一未被探索的问题提供了系统化诊断工具，推动文生图模型在局部文本控制方面的进步。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models have become increasingly capable of rendering explicit text, but reliable localized text control requires more than generating the correct string. In applications such as product labeling, signage, and interface design, target text should be rendered within a designated text-bearing region without altering the predefined subject identity or surrounding scene semantics. We refer to violations of this requirement as target-text-associated semantic leakage, in which target-text semantics are expressed through non-textual visual content beyond the designated anchor. Existing visual-text benchmarks primarily evaluate readability, spelling accuracy, and layout, leaving this form of semantic leakage largely unexamined. We introduce T2LSC-Bench, a controlled diagnostic benchmark comprising 50 seed subjects and 1,200 prompt cases per model, yielding 7,160 evaluated images across six models. Its factorized design varies semantic relation, scene openness, prompt mode, and language. A dual-branch protocol combines OCR-VLM text verification with structured VLM semantic judgments to measure Text-at-Anchor Accuracy (TAA), Semantic Subject Preservation (SSP), Semantic Leakage Rate (SLR), and Conditional Semantic Leakage Rate (cSLR). Under stress-test conditions, SLR increases from 1.2% to 18.1% and cSLR from 1.3% to 18.2%, whereas TAA decreases only from 91.4% to 90.9%. Anti-leakage prompting reduces SLR from 16.6% to 8.4% without degrading rendering accuracy. Human validation on 420 images shows strong agreement between automatic and adjudicated annotations. These results show that accurate text rendering does not guarantee local containment of target-text semantics.

</details>


### [59] [MAOL: Morphology-Aware Ordinal Learning for Fine-Grained Industrial Defect Severity Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02266)
*Zhaoyang Wang,Haiyong Chen,Binyi Su,Kun Liu,Kun Wang,Xianen Zhou,Atik Shahariar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MAOL框架，一种基于形态学感知的序数学习方法，专门用于工业缺陷的细粒度严重程度分级。MAOL将严重度分级建模为实例级序数学习任务，整合显式形态学特征，引入类别条件自适应序数阈值，并通过定位扰动进行预测感知训练。实验表明其在干净ROI和预测实例设置下均优于现有基线，并在IDA 2026挑战赛中排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度缺陷严重程度分级对工业检测至关重要，但面临三大挑战：严重度标签的序数本质、对形态学相关线索的强依赖，以及两阶段流程中干净标注实例与噪声预测实例之间的训练-测试分布差异。

Method: MAOL包含四个关键组件：(1) 将严重度分级形式化为实例级序数学习任务；(2) 引入显式形态学特征增强表征学习；(3) 提出类别条件自适应序数阈值来建模特定缺陷的分级边界；(4) 通过定位扰动实现预测感知训练，增强对不完美预测实例的鲁棒性。

Result: 在干净ROI和预测实例两种设置下的大量实验表明，MAOL在规则方法、名义分类模型和现有序数基线上均取得一致更优的性能，尤其在预测实例设置下优势更明显。该方法在IDA 2026挑战赛中获得第三名。

Conclusion: 通过序数学习与形态学感知的结合以及对训练-测试分布差异的针对性设计，MAOL有效提升了细粒度缺陷严重度分级的准确性和鲁棒性，验证了其在工业检测两阶段流水线中的实用价值。

Abstract: Fine-grained defect severity grading is essential for industrial inspection, yet remains challenging due to the ordinal nature of severity labels, the strong dependence on morphology-related cues, and the train-test discrepancy between clean annotated instances and noisy predicted instances in two-stage pipelines. We propose MAOL, a Morphology-Aware Ordinal Learning framework for fine-grained industrial defect severity grading. MAOL formulates severity grading as an instance-level ordinal learning task, incorporates explicit morphological features to enhance representation learning, introduces class-conditional adaptive ordinal thresholds to model defect-specific grading boundaries, and employs prediction-aware training via localization perturbation to improve robustness to imperfect predicted instances. Extensive experiments under both clean-ROI and predicted-instance settings demonstrate that MAOL consistently outperforms rule-based methods, nominal classification models, and existing ordinal baselines, especially in the predicted-instance setting. The proposed approach ranked third in the IDA 2026 Challenge on Fine-Grained Severity Grading for High-Precision Manufacturing.

</details>


### [60] [RouteGraph-Mona: Confusion-Aware Routing Fine-Tuning for Mineral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02282)
*Jierui Li,Zhiyuan Qi,Hao Zhu,Yufan Liu,Jixian Liu,Shaojie Jiang,Jianda Wang,Yaqi Liu,Xiaotong Li,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RouteGraph-Mona是一种基于多认知视觉适配器(Mona)改进的矿物图像分类方法，通过样本自适应路由替代静态多尺度聚合，并利用类级路由锚点和混淆加权边际进行路由空间正则化，在三个公共矿物数据集上优于Mona且与主流微调方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿物图像分类中类内外观差异大、类间视觉相似性高，现有方法Mona使用静态多尺度聚合，无法适应样本特定的尺度偏好，且难以区分视觉相似的矿物类别。因此需要改进Mona使其具备样本自适应能力和更强的类别区分能力。

Method: 提出RouteGraph-Mona：(1) 用样本自适应路由替换Mona的静态多尺度聚合，形成紧凑路由空间以捕捉图像尺度偏好；(2) 通过类级路由锚点(class-wise route anchors)正则化路由签名，鼓励类内一致的路由模式；(3) 引入混淆加权边际(confusion-weighted margins)提升视觉相似类别在路由空间中的分离度。

Result: 在三个公共矿物图像数据集上结合两种视觉骨干网络的实验中，RouteGraph-Mona在平均准确率上持续优于Mona，并与代表性微调方法和矿物图像分类基线方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 通过将静态聚合改为自适应路由并引入路由空间正则化，RouteGraph-Mona有效解决了矿物图像分类中尺度偏好差异和类别混淆问题，验证了路由空间正则化方法在参数高效微调适配器中的有效性。

Abstract: Mineral image classification is important for geological exploration and resource development, but it remains challenging due to substantial intra-class variations in appearance and high inter-class visual similarity. Multi-cognitive Visual Adapter (Mona) is a vision-oriented parameter-efficient adapter that adapts pre-trained visual models by tuning only a few parameters. However, Mona statically aggregates responses from multiple scales, limiting its ability to accommodate sample-specific scale preferences and model confusion among visually similar mineral categories. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{RouteGraph-Mona}, a lightweight route-space regularization method built on Mona. Specifically, we replace Mona's static multi-scale aggregation with sample-adaptive routing. The resulting branch-selection behavior defines a compact routing space that captures each image's scale preferences. We then regularize the resulting routing signatures with class-wise route anchors and confusion-weighted margins. The route anchors encourage class-consistent routing patterns, while the margins promote greater separation between visually similar categories in the routing space. Experiments on three public mineral image datasets with two visual backbones show that RouteGraph-Mona consistently outperforms Mona in mean accuracy and remains competitive with representative fine-tuning methods and mineral image classification baselines.

</details>


### [61] [Diffusion-Encoding Gaussian Field for Joint k-q dMRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02288)
*Zhibo Chen,Yajuan Huang,Yu Guan,Qiuyun Fan,Dong Liang,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于subject-specific空间-角度高斯场的自监督联合k-q空间扩散MRI重建方法。该方法利用共享的3D高斯图元提供局部空间支撑，每个图元携带连续q条件张量残差响应，通过渐进优化从欠采样的k空间测量中重建未观测方向的扩散加权图像，无需全采样目标或留出方向监督。在三个HCP扩散壳层和多种加速设置下，该方法在缺失方向DWI重建、张量衍生指标和主扩散方向估计上均取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散MRI需要在多个扩散编码方向上重复采集k空间数据，采集时间取决于空间和角度采样。现有的联合k-q方法要么将方向参数与固定体素关联，要么将空间重建与角度补全分离，未能充分利用不同扩散方向共享解剖结构与方向依赖信号变化之间的互补性，导致残余空间误差可能被误解为真实的角度变化并传播到未观测方向。

Method: 提出一个subject-specific的空间-角度高斯场（Spatial-Angular Gaussian Field）用于自监督联合k-q dMRI重建。具体而言：(1) 共享3D高斯图元提供局部空间支持；(2) 每个图元携带一个连续的q条件张量残差响应；(3) 每个位置的信号由多个重叠图元响应合成，从而耦合相邻空间区域和扩散方向；(4) 该场在无全采样目标或无留出方向监督的条件下，从观测方向的欠采样k空间测量中渐进优化。

Result: 在三个HCP扩散壳层上、多种加速设置下的实验中，该方法在缺失方向DWI重建、张量衍生指标以及主扩散方向（principal diffusion orientation）估计方面均表现出持续一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模共享解剖组织与方向依赖信号变化之间的互补关系，subject-specific空间-角度高斯场能够有效避免空间残余误差被误判为角度变化的传播问题，为联合k-q空间dMRI重建提供了一种自监督且无需全采样标签的可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI requires repeated k-space acquisitions over multiple diffusion-encoding directions, making acquisition time dependent on both spatial and angular sampling. Existing joint k-q methods either associate directional parameters with fixed voxels or separate spatial reconstruction from angular completion. However, diffusion-weighted images acquired under different directions share the same anatomical organization, while their local signal intensities vary with diffusion encoding. Existing formulations do not fully exploit the complementarity between shared anatomy and direction-dependent signal variation. Consequently, residual spatial errors may be misinterpreted as genuine angular variation and propagated to unobserved directions.
  We propose a subject-specific spatial-angular Gaussian field for self-supervised joint k-q dMRI reconstruction. Shared 3D Gaussian primitives provide local spatial support, with each primitive carrying a continuous q-conditioned tensor-residual response. The signal at each location is synthesized from multiple overlapping primitive responses, coupling neighboring spatial regions and diffusion directions. The field is progressively optimized from undersampled k-space measurements of observed directions, without fully sampled targets or held-out-direction supervision.
  Experiments on three HCP diffusion shells under multiple acceleration settings demonstrated consistent improvements in missing-direction DWI reconstruction, tensor-derived metrics, and principal diffusion orientation estimation.

</details>


### [62] [If It Moves, Radar Knows: A Physics-Aware Radar Transformer for Class-Agnostic Moving-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02289)
*Yinghao Sun,Shuguang Li,Jinliang Shao,Tieshan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PART（Physics-Aware Radar Transformer），一种全稀疏、仅依赖雷达的检测器，用于在封闭集标注之外检测罕见移动物体。PART预测存在置信度、代表性表面点和二维地面平面速度，通过Doppler-Aware Query Initialization (DAQI)和Physics-Guided Cross-Attention (PGCA)实现物理感知的查询-目标关联，并用不确定性感知监督处理标注噪声。以仅110万参数在nuScenes上取得0.8827的类无关平均精度（CA-AP），表面平移误差0.3188m，速度误差0.8084m/s，对标准评估之外的罕见安全关键类别召回率达0.9203，且在夜间、雨中等恶劣条件下保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭集标注训练的检测器无法检测训练类别之外的罕见移动物体，这对自动驾驶安全构成隐患。传统雷达检测方法难以在稀疏、有噪声的雷达点云上实现类感知的3D框检测，而表面位置和速度信息对运动推理和避障仍足够有用。

Method: PART由三个关键组件构成：(1) Doppler-Aware Query Initialization (DAQI)：用位置和速度聚类雷达回波生成输入相关的查询提案，替代场景无关的学习式查询；(2) Physics-Guided Cross-Attention (PGCA)：将径向多普勒一致性和雷达散射截面（RCS）融入查询-点关联机制；(3) 不确定性感知监督：随机遮蔽真值对象，并为模糊的雷达支撑查询分配软存在目标，降低对穷举标注的依赖。网络为全稀疏、仅雷达的Transformer检测器，预测存在置信度、代表性表面点和2D地面平面速度。

Result: PART仅用110万参数在nuScenes上达到0.8827的CA-AP、0.3188m的mASTE和0.8084m/s的mAVE。对标准评估中排除的罕见安全关键类别召回率达0.9203，在夜间、雨天和严重遮挡下仍保持有效。对明显假阳性的检查发现部分预测对应nuScenes标注中缺失的真实移动物体，证明了方法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PART证明利用雷达的物理特性（多普勒速度、RCS）和输入相关的查询初始化，可以在不依赖3D框几何完整恢复的前提下，以极小的模型规模实现对封闭集外罕见移动物体的可靠检测，为自动驾驶的安全感知提供了一种轻量且鲁棒的雷达-only方案。

Abstract: Detectors trained on closed-set annotations can miss rare moving objects outside the training taxonomy. Automotive radar provides category-independent Doppler motion cues and is less affected by adverse illumination and weather, but sparse, noisy returns hinder class-aware 3D box detection. Surface location and velocity remain useful for motion reasoning and collision avoidance when full box geometry is difficult to recover. We present the Physics-Aware Radar Transformer (PART), a fully sparse radar-only detector that predicts existence confidence, a representative surface point, and 2D ground-plane velocity for each moving-object hypothesis. Doppler-Aware Query Initialization (DAQI) replaces scene-independent learned queries with input-dependent proposals by clustering radar returns in position and velocity, easing query-object assignment in sparse scenes. Physics-Guided Cross-Attention (PGCA) incorporates radial-Doppler consistency and radar cross section (RCS) into query-point association. Uncertainty-aware supervision randomly masks ground-truth objects and assigns soft existence targets to ambiguous radar-supported queries, reducing reliance on exhaustive annotations. With only 1.1 million parameters, PART achieves a class-agnostic average precision (CA-AP) of 0.8827, a mean average surface translation error (mASTE) of 0.3188 m, and a mean average velocity error (mAVE) of 0.8084 m/s on nuScenes. It attains 0.9203 recall on rare and safety-relevant categories excluded from the standard evaluation and remains effective at night, in rain, and under severe occlusion. Inspection of apparent false positives shows that some predictions correspond to moving objects absent from the nuScenes annotations. Code and pretrained model weights will be publicly available at https://github.com/sunyinghao-uestc/PART.

</details>


### [63] [VoRTeC: Taming Foundation Flow for One-step Real time Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02291)
*Yichong Xia,Qinhong Wu,Qinhong Wu,Jinpeng Wang,Zeyuan Chen,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoRTeC是一个基于基础流模型(Wan2.1)的超低码率视频压缩框架，通过紧凑编码潜在视频表示、预测压缩表示沿流轨迹的位置以及整合多尺度先验来实现单步解码，相比先前扩散方法减少58%的比特消耗，解码速度提升3-197倍（720p下13 FPS，480p下32 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低码率视频压缩面临关键挑战：传统神经视频压缩必然引入模糊伪影，而基于扩散的生成式视频压缩则存在解码延迟过高和时序一致性差的问题。

Method: VoRTeC基于基础流模型Wan2.1构建，通过紧凑编码潜在视频表示、预测压缩表示沿流轨迹的位置，并整合多尺度先验，使压缩器有效利用生成式视频流先验。该方法无需访问流匹配网络的参数或梯度，即可实现一步解码和高感知保真度的重建。同时通过尾帧复用和先验缓存维持帧组间的一致性。

Result: 相比先前的扩散方法，VoRTeC减少了58%的比特消耗，解码速度提升3至197倍，在720p分辨率下达到13 FPS，在480p分辨率下达到32 FPS的解码速度。

Conclusion: VoRTeC通过利用基础流模型的生成先验，同时解决扩散生成式视频压缩的解码延迟和时序一致性问题，在超低码率下实现高效、高保真的视频压缩，验证了基于流模型的生成式压缩范式在速度和率失真性能上的显著优势。

Abstract: Ultra-low bitrate video compression still faces critical challenges: traditional neural video compression inevitably introduces blurring artifacts, while diffusion-based generative video compression suffers from excessive decoding latency and poor temporal consistency. To address these issues, we propose $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$, a Video Compression framework built upon a foundational flow model (Wan2.1). By compactly encoding latent video representations, predicting the positions of compressed representations along flow trajectories, and integrating multi-scale priors, $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$ enables the compressor to harness generative video flow priors effectively. Without accessing the parameters or gradients of flow matching networks, our framework achieves one-step decoding and reconstructions with high perceptual fidelity. Meanwhile, we maintain consistency across frame groups via tail-frame reuse and prior caching. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method reduces bit consumption by 58\% compared to prior diffusion-based approaches, with decoding speed boosted by 3 to 197 times: $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$ achieves a decoding speed of 13 FPS at 720p and 32 FPS at 480p.

</details>


### [64] [Domain shift-robust object detection with GenAI image editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02299)
*Isabel D. Stein,Thijs A. Eker,Sebastiaan P. Snel,Ella P. Fokkinga,Klamer Schutte,Luca Ambrogioni,Friso G. Heslinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用基于扩散模型的生成式图像编辑方法，通过合成模拟域偏移（如给训练数据添加伪装）来提升目标检测器在域偏移场景下的鲁棒性。在军事车辆伪装检测任务中，生成式伪装数据增强显著提升了目标检测器在真实测试图像上的mAP表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器在遇到光照、天气或遮挡等域偏移时性能显著下降，这源于模型依赖训练分布中学到的视觉捷径而非普适特征。在专业、低数据场景中获取足够多样化的真实样本十分困难。虽然扩散模型的图像编辑已被证明可以提升检测器的域内性能，但其改善域外鲁棒性的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出利用扩散式生成图像编辑在训练数据中模拟受控域偏移，以弥合源域与目标域之间的差距。具体采用两种扩散编辑模型（Qwen Image Edit 2509和Flux.2 Dev）以及LoRA微调版本的Qwen，将伪装效果合成添加到训练数据中；以非生成的黑色遮挡条作为增强质量的下界基线；使用GroundingDINO检测器在真实和合成数据上训练，涵盖15种车辆的近距离地面图像，包括树叶、网和等多光谱伪装场景。

Result: 生成式伪装增强在叶状伪装上mAP提升+20.1，网状伪装上mAP提升+14.4。多光谱伪装的生成更具挑战性，但LoRA微调在未伪装基线上提升了4.4 mAP。

Conclusion: 扩散式生成图像编辑能够有效模拟训练数据中的受控域偏移，显著改善检测器在目标域（如伪装车辆场景）的鲁棒性；对于较难生成的高保真伪装类型（多光谱），LoRA微调可进一步赋能生成模型的适应能力。

Abstract: Object detectors often degrade under domain shifts such as changes in lighting, weather, or occlusion. These shifts alter object appearance and expose a reliance on visual shortcuts learned from the training distribution that do not generalize across domains. Acquiring sufficient real-world samples to capture such domain variation is particularly difficult in specialized, low-data settings. Recent advances in diffusion-based generative image editing have shown promise for improving the in-domain performance of object detectors through synthetic data augmentation. However, their potential to improve out-of-domain robustness remains largely unexplored. We hypothesize that generative image editing can simulate a controlled domain shift in training data, effectively bridging the gap between source and target domains. To test this, we studied camouflaged military vehicle detection as a challenging domain shift scenario. Detectors trained on uncamouflaged data demonstrate substantial degradation on real test imagery containing foliage, netting, and multi-spectral camouflage across 15 vehicle classes in close-up, ground-level imagery. We used two diffusion-based editing models, Qwen Image Edit 2509 and Flux.2 Dev, to synthetically add camouflage to the training data, alongside a LoRA fine-tuned version of Qwen. A non-generative black-bar occlusion baseline served as a lower bound on augmentation quality. Using a GroundingDINO detector trained on real and synthetic data, generative camouflage augmentation yielded substantial mAP improvements for foliage (+20.1) and netting (+14.4) camouflage. Generating multi-spectral camouflage proved more challenging, but LoRA fine-tuning improved performance by 4.4 mAP over the uncamouflaged baseline.

</details>


### [65] [YesTrack: Referring Multi-Object Tracking via MLLM-based Yes/No Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02318)
*Quansheng Hu,Qin Sun,Qiansen Dai,Jin Ding,Wan Zhang,Xue Zhou,Jianxiao Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YesTrack是一种新型两阶段指代多目标跟踪（RMOT）方法，将指代任务重新表述为判别式任务，直接利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）进行Yes/No验证而非文本生成，并引入两个轻量级时间一致性约束（TCP和TRP）来提升验证的可靠性和效率。在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2上超过了现有最先进方法，即使使用最小的Qwen3-VL变体也保持高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMOT方法虽然集成了多模态大语言模型以增强泛化能力，但大多仅将其用作字幕生成器，仍需外部模块进行最终决策。这种范式不仅引入额外延迟，还严重低估了MLLMs固有的视觉-语言对齐能力。作者旨在通过判别式任务设计直接利用MLLMs的验证能力，消除外部模块依赖。

Method: YesTrack采用两阶段架构，将指代跟踪从生成式任务重构为判别式Yes/No验证任务，无需显式文本生成。为增强验证的可靠性与效率，引入了两个轻量级时间一致性约束：Temporal Confidence Prior（TCP）利用历史时间信息提供置信度先验，Temporal Reference Propagation（TRP）在时间维度上传播指代参考信息。此外还提出了YesTrack-MOT用于通用多目标跟踪，验证该判别式范式的泛化性。

Result: 在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2基准上，YesTrack显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持高推理效率，即使仅采用最小规模的Qwen3-VL模型也能实现领先性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将MLLMs从字幕生成器转变为直接判别式验证器是RMOT的一种有效范式，能够充分利用其视觉-语言对齐能力，同时通过时间一致性约束保证了效率和可靠性。研究还表明该判别式范式可迁移至通用的多目标跟踪任务。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) aims to track every instance in a video that matches a given language expression. Despite the recent integration of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to enhance generalization, existing methods predominantly relegate them to the role of caption generators, necessitating external modules for final decision-making. This paradigm not only introduces extra latency but also severely underutilizes the inherent vision-language alignment capabilities of MLLMs. To address these limitations, we propose YesTrack, a novel two-stage RMOT method that reformulates referring as a discriminative task, directly leveraging MLLMs for Yes/No verification without explicit text generation. To further enhance the reliability and efficiency of this MLLM-based verification, we introduce two lightweight temporal consistency constraints: Temporal Confidence Prior (TCP) and Temporal Reference Propagation (TRP). We further validate the generality of this discriminative paradigm by proposing YesTrack-MOT, a straightforward yet highly effective instantiation for generic multi-object tracking (MOT). Experiments on Refer-KITTI and Refer-KITTI-V2 show that YesTrack significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods while maintaining high efficiency, even when implemented with the smallest variant of Qwen3-VL. Code is released at https://github.com/ggbondrighthere24/YesTrack.

</details>


### [66] [ORB-SVM : An Innovative Hybrid Framework for Efficient Brain Tumor Detection from MRI Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02333)
*Amirhosein Azarpour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将ORB特征提取算法与SVM分类器相结合的混合框架用于脑癌MRI图像诊断，实现了约99.5%的数据缩减，在Br35H数据集上达到97.5%的分类准确率，为医学图像分析提供了一种低计算成本、资源高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑癌早期诊断的准确性对患者生存至关重要，但MRI图像的解读常受主观差异和图像噪声的干扰。现有深度学习方法虽然性能优异，但参数规模大、计算成本高且需要大量训练数据，在临床资源有限的场景下难以部署。

Method: 提出混合框架，采用ORB（Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF）算法进行精确的局部特征提取，结合SVM（支持向量机）作为核分类器进行分类。该框架实现约99.5%的数据缩减，去除冗余的背景信息，同时保留关键的肿瘤诊断特征模式，以平衡特征稀疏性与分类器鲁棒性。

Result: 在Br35H数据集上的实验评估显示，该框架实现了97.5%的分类准确率，证明了局部特征表示与优化分类器结合的有效性。
